## Video

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `video` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `media` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `video` `.rp-video` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A native `<video>` with controls. With `autoplay` it is a muted, looping background video that still shows controls so that the shopper can pause it.

- Use when: showing a hosted video file (a Shopify video's sources, or one file URL).
- Do not use when: embedding YouTube or Vimeo (write the provider's `<iframe>`), or showing a still (use Image).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Video` | `blocks/video.liquid` | `video` | `rp-video` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: children MUST be `<source>` and `<track>` elements only. Either `src` or at least one `<source>` child MUST be present.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `src` | `src` | url | URL of one video file | none (omitted) | no | no | `src` |
| `poster` | `poster` | url | URL of a still image | none (omitted) | no | no | `poster` |
| `autoplay` | `autoplay` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | when true, the three attributes `autoplay`, `muted`, `loop` |
| `width` | `width` | number | intrinsic width in px | none (omitted) | no | no | `width` |
| `height` | `height` | number | intrinsic height in px | none (omitted) | no | no | `height` |
| `ratio` | `ratio` | enum | `auto 1/1 4/5 3/4 2/3 4/3 3/2 16/9 21/9` | `auto` | no | no | `data-ratio` |
| `fit` | `fit` | enum | `cover contain` | `cover` | no | no | `data-fit` |
| `radius` | `radius` | enum | `none sm md lg xl full` | `none` | no | no | `data-radius` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-label` |

Always printed, with no prop: `controls`, `playsinline`, `preload="metadata"`.

### Content model

`flow` - restricted to `<source src type>` and `<track>` elements written by the caller. This is how a Shopify video's several sources are passed (the caller loops over them).

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (`src="https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4"`):

```html
<video class="rp-video" src="https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4" controls playsinline preload="metadata" data-ratio="auto" data-fit="cover" data-radius="none"></video>
```

Fixture `background` (`poster="https://cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0000/0001/files/lookbook.jpg"`, `autoplay`, `width=1920`, `height=1080`, `ratio="16/9"`, `radius="lg"`, `ariaLabel="Lookbook film"`; children two `<source>` elements):

```html
<video class="rp-video" poster="https://cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0000/0001/files/lookbook.jpg" width="1920" height="1080" autoplay muted loop controls playsinline preload="metadata" data-ratio="16/9" data-fit="cover" data-radius="lg" aria-label="Lookbook film"><source src="https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.webm" type="video/webm"><source src="https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4" type="video/mp4"></video>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{ "src": "https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4" }` | absent |
| `background` | `{ "poster": "https://cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0000/0001/files/lookbook.jpg", "autoplay": true, "width": 1920, "height": 1080, "ratio": "16/9", "radius": "lg", "ariaLabel": "Lookbook film" }` | `<source src="https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.webm" type="video/webm"><source src="https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4" type="video/mp4">` |
| `contain` | `{ "src": "https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4", "fit": "contain" }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "src": "https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4", "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "src": "https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4", "ratio": "bogus" }` (output `data-ratio="auto"`) | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "src": "https://cdn.shopify.com/videos/c/o/v/lookbook.mp4", "ariaLabel": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: None. (URLs are literal props and are printed unchanged by every host.)

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `autoplay`, `muted`, `loop` | root | server | `autoplay` is true | present, or absent |
| `controls`, `playsinline` | root | server | always | present |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: native controls play, pause, seek and mute. `autoplay` works natively because the video is muted.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<video controls>`; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: `ariaLabel`; SHOULD be passed. A video with speech MUST have a `<track kind="captions">` child.
- Focus: the native controls are focusable.
- Announcements: None.
- An autoplaying video always has `controls`, which is the pause mechanism required by WCAG 2.2.2. It is always `muted`.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| Space | play or pause (native, when the video has focus) |

### Styling

- File: `components/video/video.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--color-muted`, `--radius-sm`, `--radius-md`, `--radius-lg`, `--radius-xl`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-video-radius` | `0` | corner radius |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-video[data-ratio="<value>"]`, one rule for each of `1/1 4/5 3/4 2/3 4/3 3/2 16/9 21/9` | `--_ratio: <a> / <b>` (the same eight values as Image) |
| `.rp-video[data-fit="contain"]` | `--_fit: contain` |
| `.rp-video[data-radius="sm"]` / `"md"` / `"lg"` / `"xl"` / `"full"` (five rules) | `--rp-video-radius: var(--radius-sm)` / `var(--radius-md)` / `var(--radius-lg)` / `var(--radius-xl)` / `9999px` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-video` | custom properties: `--rp-video-radius` as above; `--_ratio: auto; --_fit: cover`. Declarations: `display: block; inline-size: 100%; max-inline-size: 100%; block-size: auto; aspect-ratio: var(--_ratio); object-fit: var(--_fit); border-radius: var(--rp-video-radius); background-color: var(--color-muted)` |

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Video(props: VideoProps)`; generated; JSX props `autoPlay`, `muted`, `loop`, `controls`, `playsInline`, `preload`.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: the caller loops over a video drop's sources inside the block body. Schema settings: `ratio`, `fit`, `radius`. Locale keys: `ripple.video.name`.

```tsx
<Video poster={video.previewImage.url} autoplay ratio="16/9" ariaLabel="Lookbook film">
  {video.sources.map((source) => (
    <source key={source.url} src={source.url} type={source.mimeType} />
  ))}
</Video>
```

```liquid
{% block 'video', poster: poster_url, autoplay: true, ratio: '16/9', aria_label: 'Lookbook film' %}
  {% for source in video.sources %}
    <source src="{{ source.url }}" type="{{ source.mime_type }}">
  {% endfor %}
{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4. Manifest `rules[]`: `video-needs-source` (RP113, error): a `.rp-video` element has a `src` attribute or at least one `source` child; `video-children`: kind `max-count`, selector `:scope > *:not(source):not(track)`, count `0`. Message: "A Video has a child that is not source or track." Fix: "Keep only source and track elements inside Video."

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 2: fixture `background` has the attributes `autoplay`, `muted`, `loop`, `controls`, `playsinline` in all four hosts; fixture `default` has `controls` and `playsinline` and none of `autoplay`, `muted`, `loop`.
3. Tier 5: fixture `background` in a 640 px parent has a bounding box of 640 by 360 px; the element's `muted` property is `true`.
4. Tier 6: with JavaScript disabled, fixture `default` shows native controls (the element's `controls` property is `true`).

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/theme/blocks/video.liquid`; `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` section 6.3 (`ratio`, `fit`, `radius`).
- UNVERIFIED: that React 19 server rendering prints the `muted` attribute (React sets `muted` as a property on the client). CA-4 on fixture `background` decides it; if React omits it, add the mask `{ "select": "video", "op": "drop-attr", "name": "muted" }` to fixture `background` for the `react` and `remix` hosts and rely on a tier 5 test that the element's `muted` property is `true` after load. Not attached to a spike (local test).

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.

## Icon

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `icon` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `media` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `svg` `.rp-icon` |
| Liquid tier | `snippet` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A decorative inline SVG icon from Ripple's own set of 24 icons (section 0.6), drawn in the current text colour (D-068).

- Use when: adding an icon next to text inside a Button, Link, Badge or Text.
- Do not use when: the icon is the only content of a control (use IconButton, which requires an accessible name), or a custom SVG is needed (write the `<svg>` by hand with `class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"`).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Icon` | `snippets/rp-icon.liquid` (no block file) | `svg` | `rp-icon` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: none. Icon is phrasing content and may be placed in any `text` content model.

### Props

Icon is the one component that does NOT take the universal props `id`, `span`, `hidden`, rest props or `attributes`. It takes `name` and `class` only.

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `name` | enum | `account arrow-left arrow-right cart check chevron-down chevron-left chevron-right chevron-up close filter globe info menu minus pause play plus search star tag trash warning zoom-in` | none | yes | no | selects the inner markup (section 0.6); no attribute |
| `class` (`className` in React) | `class` | string | class names | none | no | no | appended after `rp-icon` |

A `name` that is not in the list prints NOTHING (no `<svg>`), in every host; the checker reports RP101.

### Content model

`none` - the inner markup comes from `icons/<name>.svg`.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (`name="cart"`):

```html
<svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 4h2l2.4 11h10.2L20 7H6.2"/><circle cx="9" cy="19" r="1.5"/><circle cx="17" cy="19" r="1.5"/></svg>
```

Fixture `close` (`name="close"`):

```html
<svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg>
```

Fixture `unknown-name` (`name="bogus"`): the reference HTML is the empty string.

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{ "name": "cart" }` | absent |
| `close` | `{ "name": "close" }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "name": "cart", "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `unknown-name` | `{ "name": "bogus" }` | absent |

In addition the generator MUST emit one fixture per icon name, named `icon-<name>` (24 fixtures), with props `{ "name": "<name>" }`, so that conformance covers the whole set. `invalid-enum-falls-back` does not apply (`name` has no default); `unknown-name` takes its place.

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

None.

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Identical to the behaviour with JavaScript: inline SVG.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: decorative image; `checkPattern`: none. `aria-hidden="true"` and `focusable="false"` are always printed (`02-html-contract.md` A-7).
- Accessible name: none, by design. The name belongs to the control or text around the icon.
- Focus: not focusable.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/icon/icon.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: none.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-icon-size` | `1.25em` | inline and block size |
| `--rp-icon-stroke` | `1.5` | stroke width in viewBox units |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| none | |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-icon` | `display: inline-block; flex-shrink: 0; inline-size: var(--rp-icon-size); block-size: var(--rp-icon-size); vertical-align: middle; fill: none; stroke: currentColor; stroke-width: var(--rp-icon-stroke); stroke-linecap: round; stroke-linejoin: round; pointer-events: none` |

Icon does not mirror in right-to-left. A component that embeds a directional icon mirrors it on its own part class (see Breadcrumb).

### Host notes

- HTML: copy the `<svg>` from the reference file.
- React: `export function Icon(props: IconProps)`; generated; the inner markup is a generated constant map from name to JSX; JSX attributes `viewBox`, `aria-hidden="true"`, `focusable="false"`.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% render 'rp-icon', name: 'cart' %}`. The snippet is a generated `{% case name %}` with one `{% when %}` per icon and no `{% else %}`. It can be rendered from templates, blocks and snippets. No schema. Locale keys: none.

```tsx
<Button><Icon name="plus" />Add to cart</Button>
```

```liquid
{% block 'button', type: 'submit' %}{% render 'rp-icon', name: 'plus' %}Add to cart{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree` (hidden from it by `aria-hidden`); actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4 (RP101 for an unknown `name`). Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 1: the file names in `icons/` are exactly the 24 names of section 0.6 with extension `.svg`; each file matches `^<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24">.+</svg>\n?$` and contains no `fill`, `stroke`, `style`, `class` or `id` attribute.
3. Tier 2: the 24 `icon-<name>` fixtures are canonically equal in all four hosts; `unknown-name` renders the empty string in all four hosts.
4. Tier 5: fixture `default` inside `<span style="font-size:16px;color:rgb(1,2,3)">` has a bounding box of 20 by 20 px and computes `stroke: rgb(1, 2, 3)`, `fill: none`.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-068 as amended (Icon takes only `name` and `class`; an unknown name prints nothing; path data defined in section 0.6 of this document), section 6.6; `ripple/theme/assets/icon-*.svg` (14 of the names); `ripple/docs/research/base-theme.md` (icon button costs three statements; Icon as a snippet); `02-html-contract.md` section 12.6 row 14 (canonical form of an icon).
- UNVERIFIED: None.

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.

## Placeholder

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `placeholder` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `media` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `div` `.rp-placeholder` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A neutral box with a fixed aspect ratio that stands in for a missing image, so that grids keep their shape.

- Use when: a product, collection or article has no image.
- Do not use when: content is still loading (use Skeleton).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Placeholder` | `blocks/placeholder.liquid` | `div` | `rp-placeholder` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: none.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ratio` | `ratio` | enum | `1/1 4/5 3/4 2/3 4/3 3/2 16/9 21/9` | `1/1` | no | no | `data-ratio` |
| `radius` | `radius` | enum | `none sm md lg xl full` | `none` | no | no | `data-radius` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none | no | no | when set: `role="img"` and `aria-label`; when unset: `aria-hidden="true"` |

### Content model

`none` - the root is always empty.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props):

```html
<div class="rp-placeholder" data-ratio="1/1" data-radius="none" aria-hidden="true"></div>
```

Fixture `labelled` (`ratio="4/5"`, `radius="lg"`, `ariaLabel="No image available"`):

```html
<div class="rp-placeholder" data-ratio="4/5" data-radius="lg" role="img" aria-label="No image available"></div>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | absent |
| `labelled` | `{ "ratio": "4/5", "radius": "lg", "ariaLabel": "No image available" }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "ratio": "bogus" }` (output `data-ratio="1/1"`) | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "ariaLabel": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

None.

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Identical to the behaviour with JavaScript: static markup styled by CSS.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: decorative box, or `role="img"` when labelled; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: `ariaLabel`, optional. Without it the box is hidden from assistive technology.
- Focus: not focusable.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/placeholder/placeholder.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--color-muted`, `--color-muted-foreground`, `--radius-sm`, `--radius-md`, `--radius-lg`, `--radius-xl`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-placeholder-radius` | `0` | corner radius |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-placeholder[data-ratio="<value>"]`, one rule for each of `4/5 3/4 2/3 4/3 3/2 16/9 21/9` | `--_ratio: <a> / <b>` |
| `.rp-placeholder[data-radius="sm"]` / `"md"` / `"lg"` / `"xl"` / `"full"` (five rules) | `--rp-placeholder-radius: var(--radius-sm)` / `var(--radius-md)` / `var(--radius-lg)` / `var(--radius-xl)` / `9999px` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-placeholder` | custom properties: `--rp-placeholder-radius` as above; `--_ratio: 1 / 1`. Declarations: `display: block; inline-size: 100%; aspect-ratio: var(--_ratio); border-radius: var(--rp-placeholder-radius); background-color: var(--color-muted); background-image: linear-gradient(135deg, transparent, color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-muted-foreground) 12%, transparent))` |

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Placeholder(props: PlaceholderProps)`; generated; no `children`.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'placeholder', ratio: '4/5' %}{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: `ratio`, `radius`. Locale keys: `ripple.placeholder.name`.

```tsx
{product.featuredImage ? <Image image={product.featuredImage} ratio="4/5" /> : <Placeholder ratio="4/5" />}
```

```liquid
{% if product.featured_image %}
  {% block 'image', image: product.featured_image, ratio: '4/5' %}{% endblock %}
{% else %}
  {% block 'placeholder', ratio: '4/5' %}{% endblock %}
{% endif %}
```

None.

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

Common rules of section 0.4. Manifest `rules[]`: none.

2. Tier 5: fixture `default` in a 200 px parent has a bounding box of 200 by 200 px; fixture `labelled` in a 200 px parent has 200 by 250 px and `border-radius` 8 px.
3. Tier 2: fixture `default` has `aria-hidden="true"` and no `role`; fixture `labelled` has `role="img"`, `aria-label` and no `aria-hidden`.

## Button

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `button` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `actions` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `button`, or `a` when `href` is set, `.rp-button` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

The standard action control with a text label. It is a `<button>` for form submission and native invokers, and an `<a>` when it navigates.

- Use when: submitting a form, opening a Dialog, Drawer or Popover (`commandFor` / `popoverTarget`), or navigating with a button look (`href`).
- Do not use when: the control has only an icon (use IconButton), or the link sits in running text (use Link).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Button` | `blocks/button.liquid` (markup in `snippets/rp-button.liquid`) | `button` or `a` | `rp-button` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: MUST NOT contain interactive content. A `<button>` root MUST be useful without JavaScript: it MUST be a `type="submit"` or `type="reset"` button of a form, or have `commandFor` with `command`, or have `popoverTarget` (`02-html-contract.md` PE-2). A `type="button"` Button with none of these is allowed in two cases only: inside an `rp-*` root with `data-rp-js` passed through rest props, or as a tooltip trigger (prop `tooltip`) inside the `tooltip-trigger` part of a Tooltip (`10-components-interactive.md`; Tooltip is `noJs.level: none`, D-013). A Button composed by contract by another component keeps `rp-button` as its first class (FC-14).

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `variant` | `variant` | enum | `primary secondary outline ghost link destructive` | `primary` | no | no | `data-variant` |
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md lg` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |
| `type` | `type` | enum | `button submit reset` | `button` | no | no | `type`; `<button>` only |
| `href` | `href` | url | any URL | none (omitted) | no | no | `href`; when set the root is `<a>` |
| `target` | `target` | string | a browsing context name | none (omitted) | no | no | `target`; `<a>` only |
| `rel` | `rel` | string | link types | none (omitted) | no | no | `rel`; `<a>` only |
| `name` | `name` | string | form field name | none (omitted) | no | no | `name`; `<button>` only |
| `value` | `value` | string | form field value | none (omitted) | no | no | `value`; `<button>` only |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `<button>`: `disabled`. `<a>`: `aria-disabled="true"` and `tabindex="-1"`, `href` stays |
| `pending` | `pending` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `data-pending=""` and `aria-busy="true"` |
| `fullWidth` | `full_width` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `data-full-width=""` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-label` |
| `commandFor` | `command_for` | id | id of a `<dialog>` or `[popover]` element | none (omitted) | no | no | `commandfor`; `<button>` only |
| `command` | `command` | string | `show-modal`, `close`, `show-popover`, `hide-popover`, `toggle-popover`, or a custom command matching `^--[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$` | none (omitted) | no | no | `command`; `<button>` only |
| `popoverTarget` | `popover_target` | id | id of a `[popover]` element | none (omitted) | no | no | `popovertarget`; `<button>` only |
| `formId` | `form_id` | id | id of a `<form>` in the same document | none (omitted) | no | no | `form`; `<button>` only (ledger 6.4). Associates the button with a form elsewhere in the document |
| `dialog` | `dialog` | id | the id of a `<dialog>` in the same document | none (omitted) | no | no | `data-rp-open="<id>"`; `<a>` only: allowed only when `href` is set (D-009) |
| `tooltip` | `tooltip` | id | the `id` of the Tooltip whose trigger this button is | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-describedby="<id>-content"` and `data-slot="tooltip-trigger"`; `<button>` only: allowed only without `href` (D-082) |

Rendering rules: the root is `<a>` if and only if `href` is set. On `<a>`, the props `type`, `name`, `value`, `commandFor`, `command`, `popoverTarget`, `formId`, `tooltip` print nothing. On `<button>`, `target`, `rel` and `dialog` print nothing. `commandFor` and `command` MUST be passed together. This prop order is the manifest prop order; the complete binding files in `06-bindings.md` sections 6.4, 7.4 and 8.4 are copies that MUST follow this table (D-069).

Prop `dialog` (the opener pattern of D-009, same meaning as Link `dialog`):

1. It is used when an overlay has a page equivalent (cart drawer and `/cart`, search dialog and `/search`, mobile nav and the footer menu anchor). `href` is the page equivalent; `dialog` is the id of the `<dialog>`.
2. It prints `data-rp-open="<id>"` on the `<a>`, HTML-escaped. Callers MUST NOT pass `data-rp-open` through rest props or `attributes`.
3. The server MUST NOT print `aria-haspopup` or `aria-controls`. After upgrade the runtime's opener listener (`04-elements.md`) sets `aria-haspopup="dialog"` and `aria-controls="<id>"` on the link when the target is a `<dialog>` inside a defined `rp-dialog`. These two attributes are the global upgrade allow-list entry for `a[data-rp-open]` (`GLOBAL_UPGRADE_ALLOW`, D-071 item 2).
4. Without JavaScript the link navigates to `href`.
5. When the target dialog is not on the page (for example the CartDrawer on the cart page, D-091), the caller MUST omit `dialog`; the link then navigates.
6. `dialog` without `href` is an error: the binding prints nothing for it and the checker reports it (Checker rules).

Prop `tooltip` (D-082):

1. It is passed to the one Button or IconButton inside the `tooltip-trigger` part of a Tooltip; its value is the Tooltip's `id`.
2. It prints `aria-describedby="<id>-content"` and `data-slot="tooltip-trigger"` on the `<button>`. Callers MUST NOT pass these two attributes through rest props or `attributes`.
3. A link MUST NOT be a tooltip trigger: with `href` set, `tooltip` prints nothing (UI SDK writes `type="button"` on a link trigger; measured in `10-components-interactive.md`).
4. The button MUST have its own accessible name (text content or `ariaLabel`); the tooltip text is never the only name (D-013).
5. `tooltip` and `describedBy` do not both exist on Button: Button has no `describedBy` prop.

### Content model

`text` - the label: text, optionally with Icon elements before or after it. No interactive content.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props; children `Add to cart`):

```html
<button class="rp-button" type="button" data-variant="primary" data-size="md">Add to cart</button>
```

Fixture `link` (`href="/cart"`; children `View cart`):

```html
<a class="rp-button" href="/cart" data-variant="primary" data-size="md">View cart</a>
```

Fixture `link-disabled` (`href="/cart"`, `variant="outline"`, `disabled`):

```html
<a class="rp-button" href="/cart" data-variant="outline" data-size="md" aria-disabled="true" tabindex="-1">View cart</a>
```

Fixture `submit-pending` (`type="submit"`, `name="intent"`, `value="add"`, `size="lg"`, `fullWidth`, `pending`):

```html
<button class="rp-button" type="submit" name="intent" value="add" data-variant="primary" data-size="lg" data-full-width="" data-pending="" aria-busy="true">Add to cart</button>
```

Fixture `disabled` (`variant="outline"`, `size="sm"`, `disabled`; children `Sold out`):

```html
<button class="rp-button" type="button" disabled data-variant="outline" data-size="sm">Sold out</button>
```

Fixture `dialog-opener` (`variant="link"`, `commandFor="size-guide"`, `command="show-modal"`; children `Size guide`):

```html
<button class="rp-button" type="button" commandfor="size-guide" command="show-modal" data-variant="link" data-size="md">Size guide</button>
```

Fixture `link-opener` (`href="/cart"`, `dialog="cart-drawer"`; children `Cart`):

```html
<a class="rp-button" href="/cart" data-variant="primary" data-size="md" data-rp-open="cart-drawer">Cart</a>
```

Fixture `tooltip-trigger` (`tooltip="size-tip"`; children `Sizing`). The root MUST equal, attribute for attribute, the Button trigger printed inside the Tooltip fixtures of `10-components-interactive.md` for the same props:

```html
<button class="rp-button" type="button" data-variant="primary" data-size="md" aria-describedby="size-tip-content" data-slot="tooltip-trigger">Sizing</button>
```

Fixture `form-associated` (`type="submit"`, `formId="cart-lines-form"`, `name="checkout"`; children `Check out`):

```html
<button class="rp-button" type="submit" name="checkout" form="cart-lines-form" data-variant="primary" data-size="md">Check out</button>
```

Fixture `with-icon` (`variant="secondary"`, `popoverTarget="sort-menu"`, `ariaLabel="Sort products"`; children an Icon then text):

```html
<button class="rp-button" type="button" popovertarget="sort-menu" data-variant="secondary" data-size="md" aria-label="Sort products"><svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 6h16M7 12h10M10 18h4"/></svg>Sort</button>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `Add to cart` |
| `link` | `{ "href": "/cart" }` | `View cart` |
| `link-disabled` | `{ "href": "/cart", "variant": "outline", "disabled": true }` | `View cart` |
| `link-external` | `{ "href": "https://example.com/", "target": "_blank", "rel": "noopener", "variant": "ghost" }` | `Example` |
| `submit-pending` | `{ "type": "submit", "name": "intent", "value": "add", "size": "lg", "fullWidth": true, "pending": true }` | `Add to cart` |
| `disabled` | `{ "variant": "outline", "size": "sm", "disabled": true }` | `Sold out` |
| `dialog-opener` | `{ "variant": "link", "commandFor": "size-guide", "command": "show-modal" }` | `Size guide` |
| `with-icon` | `{ "variant": "secondary", "popoverTarget": "sort-menu", "ariaLabel": "Sort products" }` | `<svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 6h16M7 12h10M10 18h4"/></svg>Sort` |
| `destructive` | `{ "variant": "destructive", "type": "reset" }` | `Clear` |
| `link-opener` | `{ "href": "/cart", "dialog": "cart-drawer" }` | `Cart` |
| `tooltip-trigger` | `{ "tooltip": "size-tip" }` | `Sizing` |
| `form-associated` | `{ "type": "submit", "formId": "cart-lines-form", "name": "checkout" }` | `Check out` |
| `link-ignores-button-props` | `{ "href": "/cart", "tooltip": "size-tip", "formId": "cart-lines-form" }` (output equals fixture `link`) | `View cart` |
| `button-ignores-dialog` | `{ "dialog": "cart-drawer" }` (output equals fixture `default`) | `Add to cart` |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | `Add to cart` |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "variant": "bogus" }` (output `data-variant="primary"`) | `Add to cart` |
| `escaping` | `{ "href": "/cart", "target": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | `View cart` |

Fixture lint note: check `pe-2` (`02-html-contract.md` section 11.8) rejects a `type="button"` button with no invoker. The Button and IconButton fixtures `default`, `disabled`, `class`, `invalid-enum-falls-back` are such buttons by necessity (required props only, F-10). The lint MUST skip check `pe-2` for the root element of a fixture whose component id is `button` or `icon-button` (D-074 item 8). Pages are still checked by RP301.

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `disabled` | `<button>` root | server | `disabled` is true | present, or absent |
| `aria-disabled`, `tabindex` | `<a>` root | server | `disabled` is true | `true` and `-1`, or absent |
| `data-pending`, `aria-busy` | root | server | `pending` is true | `""` and `true`, or absent |
| `data-rp-open` | `<a>` root | server | `dialog` is set and `href` is set | the dialog id, or absent |
| `aria-describedby`, `data-slot` | `<button>` root | server | `tooltip` is set and `href` is unset | `<tooltip>-content` and `tooltip-trigger`, or absent |
| `aria-haspopup`, `aria-controls` | `<a>` root | runtime opener listener (`04-elements.md`) | after upgrade, on a link with `data-rp-open` whose target is a `<dialog>` inside a defined `rp-dialog` | absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): `aria-haspopup="dialog"` and `aria-controls="<dialog id>"` on an `<a>` root that has `data-rp-open`. This is the global entry for `a[data-rp-open]` (D-009, D-071 item 2; `02-html-contract.md` section 7.3 entry 1); the Button manifest entry has an empty `upgradeAllow[]`. Nothing else. (Commerce elements set `data-pending` and `aria-busy` on their own roots and forms, never on a Button; `05-commerce.md`. A tooltip trigger button inside `ui-tooltip` is covered by the Tooltip contract of `10-components-interactive.md`.)

### Behaviour with JavaScript

1. A `<button>` with `commandfor` and `command` opens or closes its target natively, or through the Invoker Commands polyfill of `04-elements.md` on browsers without the feature. Button itself has no script.
2. A `<button>` with `popovertarget` toggles its popover natively.
3. An `<a>` root with `data-rp-open` (props `href` and `dialog`): an unmodified primary click calls `showModal()` on the target dialog instead of navigating. This is done by the document-level opener listener of `04-elements.md`, not by Button; Button only prints `data-rp-open`.
4. A `<button>` root with `tooltip`: the surrounding `ui-tooltip` shows its content on hover and focus (`10-components-interactive.md`). Button only prints the two attributes.
5. A `<button>` with `form="<id>"` submits or resets the form with that id (native form association).

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional. Submit and reset buttons act on their form; links navigate; `popovertarget` works natively; `commandfor` works natively on browsers with Invoker Commands (on other browsers the button does nothing, which is why an overlay with a page equivalent MUST be opened by a Link, or by a Button or IconButton with `href`, that has the `dialog` prop, D-009). With `href` and `dialog` the link navigates to `href`. A `form` attribute works natively. A tooltip trigger shows no tooltip (Tooltip is `noJs.level: none`); the button keeps its own accessible name.

### Events, commands and methods

| Kind | Name | Target | Detail or arguments | React callback |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| command | `show-modal`, `close`, `show-popover`, `hide-popover`, `toggle-popover`, `--<custom>` | the element whose id is `commandFor` | none (native Invoker Commands; the Button is the invoker, not the receiver) | none |

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<button>` or `<a href>`; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: the text content, or `ariaLabel`. REQUIRED (RP201).
- Focus: focusable natively. A disabled `<a>` root leaves the tab order. A pending button stays focusable and is not disabled (D-045 item 3); `aria-busy="true"` tells assistive technology it is busy. The server MUST NOT print `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` or `aria-haspopup` on a button with `commandfor` or `popovertarget` (`02-html-contract.md` I-2).
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| Enter, Space | activates a `<button>` (native) |
| Enter | follows an `<a>` (native) |

### Styling

- File: `components/button/button.css` (layer `rp.components`). The complete, normative file is in `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 7.4 and MUST be copied from there.
- Tokens used: `--spacing-control-sm`, `--spacing-control-md`, `--spacing-control-lg`, `--spacing-target-min`, `--spacing`, `--radius-control`, `--radius-xs`, `--text-sm`, `--text-base`, `--font-weight-medium`, `--color-primary`, `--color-primary-foreground`, `--color-secondary`, `--color-secondary-foreground`, `--color-foreground`, `--color-input`, `--color-link`, `--color-destructive`, `--color-destructive-foreground`, `--rp-duration-fast`, `--rp-duration-slow`, `--ease-out`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-button-height` | `var(--spacing-control-md)` | minimum block size |
| `--rp-button-px` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 5)` | inline padding |
| `--rp-button-gap` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 2)` | gap between icon and label |
| `--rp-button-radius` | `var(--radius-control)` | corner radius |
| `--rp-button-font-size` | `var(--text-sm)` | font size |
| `--rp-button-font-weight` | `var(--font-weight-medium)` | font weight |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-button[data-variant="secondary"]`, `"outline"`, `"ghost"`, `"link"`, `"destructive"` | `--_bg`, `--_fg`, `--_border` (and for `link` the variables that remove the box); exact values in `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 7.4 |
| `.rp-button[data-size="sm"]`, `"lg"` | `--rp-button-height`, `--rp-button-px`, `--rp-button-font-size` |
| `.rp-button[data-full-width]` | `--_display: flex; --_inline-size: 100%` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| all | all | as written in `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 7.4 |

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Button(props: ButtonProps)`; generated; complete file in `06-bindings.md` section 6.4 (a copy of this contract, D-069; it MUST include `formId`, `dialog` and `tooltip`). An `onClick` handler MAY be passed through rest props; the Button MUST still satisfy the nesting rule above. `formId` is printed with the JSX prop `form`; `aria-describedby`, `data-slot` and `data-rp-open` are printed as literal attribute names after the rest-props spread.
- Remix: complete file in `06-bindings.md` section 7.4. No `data-rmx-*` attribute is printed by Button; a cart or checkout link gets `data-rmx-document` through rest props (D-034 item 3).
- Liquid: complete files in `06-bindings.md` section 8.4. Params `form_id`, `dialog`, `tooltip`. `{{ attributes }}` is printed LAST in the opening tag (D-035). Schema settings: `variant`, `size` (label keys of section 0.5). Locale keys: `ripple.button.name`.

```tsx
<Button variant="outline" commandFor="size-guide" command="show-modal">Size guide</Button>
```

```liquid
{% block 'button', variant: 'outline', command_for: 'size-guide', command: 'show-modal' %}Size guide{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none. A Button that performs an irreversible action carries `data-agent-confirm="<sentence>"` through rest props (`attributes` in Liquid); CheckoutButton (`11-components-commerce.md`) does this itself.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4, plus RP201 (accessible name), RP301 (a button inside a commerce root that does nothing without JavaScript), RP114 (the targets of `commandfor`, `popovertarget`, `form`, `data-rp-open` and `aria-describedby` exist in the document), RP302 (an overlay with `noJs.fallbackHref` is opened by an `<a href>`). Manifest `rules[]` (reported by RP113):

- `button-no-interactive-content`: kind `forbids-descendant`, selector `:scope`, target `a, button, input, select, textarea, details, label`. Message: "A Button contains interactive content." Fix: "Move the inner control out of the Button."
- `button-command-pair`: kind `requires-attribute`, selector `button.rp-button[commandfor]`, target `command`. Message: "A Button has commandfor without command." Fix: "Pass command together with commandFor."
- `button-command-needs-target`: kind `requires-attribute`, selector `button.rp-button[command]`, target `commandfor`. Message: "A Button has command without commandfor." Fix: "Pass commandFor together with command."
- `button-dialog-needs-href`: kind `requires-attribute`, selector `button.rp-button[data-rp-open]:not([commandfor])`, target `href`. Message: "A Button has the dialog prop but no href, so it does nothing without JavaScript." Fix: "Pass href with the page equivalent of the dialog, or open the dialog with commandFor and command." (A `<button>` never has `href`, so every subject is a finding. `:not([commandfor])` excludes the buttons of the SPIKE-05 fallback path.)

In these rules the subject is the component root itself; `07-manifest-registry-cli.md` section 9.5.3 MUST evaluate a compound selector against the root as well as its descendants.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: the "Values a test can assert" table of `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 7.4 holds (11 rows).
3. Tier 5: for each of the 6 variants in schemes `light` and `dark`, the contrast of the label on the fill is at least 4.5:1.
4. Tier 5: fixture `submit-pending` has the same bounding box width as the same fixture without `pending` (0.5 px), computes `color` with alpha 0, and is focusable.
5. Tier 5: fixture `link-disabled` is skipped by Tab and computes `pointer-events: none`; fixture `disabled` matches `:disabled`.
6. Tier 5 and tier 9: fixture `dialog-opener` in a page with a Dialog whose id is `size-guide`: Enter on the button opens the dialog (`dialog.open` is true) in Chromium natively and in WebKit and Gecko through the polyfill.
7. Tier 6: a `type="submit"` Button inside `<form method="get" action="/search">` submits the form with JavaScript disabled.
8. Tier 5: fixture `link-opener` in a page that contains an `rp-dialog` with `<dialog id="cart-drawer">`: after upgrade the link has `aria-haspopup="dialog"` and `aria-controls="cart-drawer"`; a click opens the dialog and the URL does not change. Tier 6: the same page with JavaScript disabled: a click navigates to `/cart`.
9. Tier 2: the canonical form of fixture `tooltip-trigger` equals the canonical form of a Button trigger of the Tooltip fixtures of `10-components-interactive.md` after replacing that fixture's tooltip id with `size-tip` and its text with `Sizing`.
10. Tier 2: fixture `link-ignores-button-props` prints no `form`, `aria-describedby` or `data-slot` attribute; fixture `button-ignores-dialog` prints no `data-rp-open` attribute.
11. Tier 6: fixture `form-associated` placed OUTSIDE `<form id="cart-lines-form" method="get" action="/x"><input name="a" value="1"></form>` submits that form with JavaScript disabled (the URL becomes `/x?a=1&checkout=`).
12. Tier 1: each manifest rule of "Checker rules" has a failing sample in `components/button/button.test.ts` that produces exactly one RP113 finding with that rule id.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-008 item 2, D-009 as amended (`dialog` on Button with `href`), D-082 (`tooltip`), D-086, D-031, sections 6.3 and 6.4 (`formId`, `dialog`, `tooltip`); `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 7.4; `06-bindings.md` sections 6.4, 7.4, 8.4; `02-html-contract.md` section 4.6; `ripple/theme/blocks/button.liquid`; `ripple/theme/snippets/command-polyfill.liquid`.
- UNVERIFIED: React 19 and Remix 3 printing `commandfor` and `command` unchanged, and the polyfill in WebKit and Gecko (SPIKE-05).

### Fallback (SPIKE-05)

If SPIKE-05 fails, a `<button>` root that has `commandFor` additionally prints one hook attribute, chosen by `command`: `data-rp-open="<commandFor>"` when `command` is `show-modal`; `data-rp-close="<commandFor>"` when `command` is `close`. Nothing else changes. The runtime's opener listener handles both (`02-html-contract.md` section 15, `06-bindings.md` section 14). Fixture `dialog-opener` then becomes:

```html
<button class="rp-button" type="button" commandfor="size-guide" command="show-modal" data-variant="link" data-size="md" data-rp-open="size-guide">Size guide</button>
```

## IconButton

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `icon-button` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `actions` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `button`, or `a` when `href` is set, `.rp-icon-button` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | `icon` |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A square action control whose only content is one icon. It always has an accessible name.

- Use when: a close button, a remove button, carousel arrows, a header icon action.
- Do not use when: the control has visible text (use Button, with an Icon in its children).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `IconButton` | `blocks/icon-button.liquid` | `button` or `a` | `rp-icon-button` | none | yes | none |
| icon | none (printed by the root from `icon`) | `snippets/rp-icon.liquid` | `svg` | `rp-icon` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: the root contains exactly one Icon and nothing else. The nesting rule of Button for `<button>` roots applies (useful without JavaScript, PE-2).

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `icon` | `icon` | enum | the 24 icon names of section 0.6 | none | yes | no | the embedded Icon (`name` = `icon`); no attribute |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none | yes | no | `aria-label` |
| `variant` | `variant` | enum | `primary secondary outline ghost link destructive` | `ghost` | no | no | `data-variant` |
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md lg` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |
| `type` | `type` | enum | `button submit reset` | `button` | no | no | `type`; `<button>` only |
| `href` | `href` | url | any URL | none (omitted) | no | no | `href`; when set the root is `<a>` |
| `target` | `target` | string | a browsing context name | none (omitted) | no | no | `target`; `<a>` only |
| `rel` | `rel` | string | link types | none (omitted) | no | no | `rel`; `<a>` only |
| `name` | `name` | string | form field name | none (omitted) | no | no | `name`; `<button>` only |
| `value` | `value` | string | form field value | none (omitted) | no | no | `value`; `<button>` only |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | as Button |
| `pending` | `pending` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `data-pending=""` and `aria-busy="true"` |
| `commandFor` | `command_for` | id | id of a `<dialog>` or `[popover]` element | none (omitted) | no | no | `commandfor`; `<button>` only |
| `command` | `command` | string | as Button | none (omitted) | no | no | `command`; `<button>` only |
| `popoverTarget` | `popover_target` | id | id of a `[popover]` element | none (omitted) | no | no | `popovertarget`; `<button>` only |
| `formId` | `form_id` | id | id of a `<form>` in the same document | none (omitted) | no | no | `form`; `<button>` only (ledger 6.4). Associates the button with a form elsewhere in the document |
| `dialog` | `dialog` | id | the id of a `<dialog>` in the same document | none (omitted) | no | no | `data-rp-open="<id>"`; `<a>` only: allowed only when `href` is set (D-009) |
| `tooltip` | `tooltip` | id | the `id` of the Tooltip whose trigger this button is | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-describedby="<id>-content"` and `data-slot="tooltip-trigger"`; `<button>` only: allowed only without `href` (D-082) |

Rendering rules: identical to Button, including the rules of the props `formId`, `dialog` (allowed only when `href` is set; prints `data-rp-open`; the six numbered rules under Button apply) and `tooltip` (allowed only without `href`; prints `aria-describedby="<id>-content"` and `data-slot="tooltip-trigger"`; the five numbered rules under Button apply). There is no `fullWidth`. `icon` and `ariaLabel` are REQUIRED (D-068 as amended).

### Content model

`none` - the content is the embedded Icon.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (`icon="close"`, `ariaLabel="Close"`):

```html
<button class="rp-icon-button" type="button" data-variant="ghost" data-size="md" aria-label="Close"><svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button>
```

Fixture `dialog-closer` (`icon="close"`, `ariaLabel="Close"`, `size="sm"`, `commandFor="size-guide"`, `command="close"`):

```html
<button class="rp-icon-button" type="button" commandfor="size-guide" command="close" data-variant="ghost" data-size="sm" aria-label="Close"><svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button>
```

Fixture `link` (`icon="account"`, `ariaLabel="Account"`, `href="/account"`, `variant="outline"`):

```html
<a class="rp-icon-button" href="/account" data-variant="outline" data-size="md" aria-label="Account"><svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4"/><path d="M4 20c0-4 3.6-6 8-6s8 2 8 6"/></svg></a>
```

Fixture `link-opener` (`icon="cart"`, `ariaLabel="Cart"`, `href="/cart"`, `dialog="cart-drawer"`):

```html
<a class="rp-icon-button" href="/cart" data-variant="ghost" data-size="md" aria-label="Cart" data-rp-open="cart-drawer"><svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 4h2l2.4 11h10.2L20 7H6.2"/><circle cx="9" cy="19" r="1.5"/><circle cx="17" cy="19" r="1.5"/></svg></a>
```

Fixture `tooltip-trigger` (`icon="info"`, `ariaLabel="Sizing information"`, `tooltip="size-tip"`). The root MUST equal the trigger button of fixture `default` of Tooltip in `10-components-interactive.md`:

```html
<button class="rp-icon-button" type="button" data-variant="ghost" data-size="md" aria-label="Sizing information" aria-describedby="size-tip-content" data-slot="tooltip-trigger"><svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 11v5M12 8h.01"/></svg></button>
```

Fixture `form-associated` (`icon="trash"`, `ariaLabel="Remove"`, `type="submit"`, `formId="cart-lines-form"`, `name="intent"`, `value="remove"`):

```html
<button class="rp-icon-button" type="submit" name="intent" value="remove" form="cart-lines-form" data-variant="ghost" data-size="md" aria-label="Remove"><svg class="rp-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7h16M10 7V4h4v3M6 7l1 13h10l1-13M10 11v6M14 11v6"/></svg></button>
```

|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{ "icon": "close", "ariaLabel": "Close" }` | absent |
| `link-opener` | `{ "icon": "cart", "ariaLabel": "Cart", "href": "/cart", "dialog": "cart-drawer" }` | absent |
| `tooltip-trigger` | `{ "icon": "info", "ariaLabel": "Sizing information", "tooltip": "size-tip" }` | absent |
| `form-associated` | `{ "icon": "trash", "ariaLabel": "Remove", "type": "submit", "formId": "cart-lines-form", "name": "intent", "value": "remove" }` | absent |
| `dialog-closer` | `{ "icon": "close", "ariaLabel": "Close", "size": "sm", "commandFor": "size-guide", "command": "close" }` | absent |

| `submit-pending` | `{ "icon": "trash", "ariaLabel": "Remove", "type": "submit", "name": "intent", "value": "remove", "variant": "destructive", "size": "lg", "pending": true }` | absent |
| `disabled` | `{ "icon": "plus", "ariaLabel": "Increase", "disabled": true, "popoverTarget": "more" }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "icon": "close", "ariaLabel": "Close", "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `disabled` | `<button>` root | server | `disabled` is true | present, or absent |
| `aria-disabled`, `tabindex` | `<a>` root | server | `disabled` is true | `true` and `-1`, or absent |
| `data-pending`, `aria-busy` | root | server | `pending` is true | `""` and `true`, or absent |
| `data-rp-open` | `<a>` root | server | `dialog` is set and `href` is set | the dialog id, or absent |
| `aria-describedby`, `data-slot` | `<button>` root | server | `tooltip` is set and `href` is unset | `<tooltip>-content` and `tooltip-trigger`, or absent |
| `aria-haspopup`, `aria-controls` | `<a>` root | runtime opener listener (`04-elements.md`) | after upgrade, on a link with `data-rp-open` whose target is a `<dialog>` inside a defined `rp-dialog` | absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): as Button: only the global entry for `a[data-rp-open]` (`aria-haspopup`, `aria-controls`); the IconButton manifest entry has an empty `upgradeAllow[]`.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

1. As Button: native invokers (`commandfor`, `popovertarget`), polyfilled where missing. IconButton itself has no script.
2. As Button: with `href` and `dialog`, an unmodified primary click opens the target dialog through the document-level opener listener of `04-elements.md`.
3. As Button: with `tooltip`, the surrounding `ui-tooltip` shows its content on hover and focus (`10-components-interactive.md`).

### Behaviour without JavaScript

As Button.

### Events, commands and methods

| Kind | Name | Target | Detail or arguments | React callback |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| command | `show-modal`, `close`, `show-popover`, `hide-popover`, `toggle-popover`, `--<custom>` | the element whose id is `commandFor` | none (native Invoker Commands) | none |

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<button>` or `<a href>`; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: `ariaLabel`, REQUIRED (D-068). A Tooltip MAY repeat it visually but is never the name.
- Focus: as Button.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| Enter, Space | activates a `<button>` (native) |
| Enter | follows an `<a>` (native) |

### Styling

- File: `components/icon-button/icon-button.css` (layer `rp.components`). It does not share rules with `button.css` (CSS-02); it repeats the colour-slot pattern.
- Tokens used: `--spacing-control-sm`, `--spacing-control-md`, `--spacing-control-lg`, `--radius-control`, `--text-base`, `--color-primary`, `--color-primary-foreground`, `--color-secondary`, `--color-secondary-foreground`, `--color-foreground`, `--color-input`, `--color-link`, `--color-destructive`, `--color-destructive-foreground`, `--rp-duration-fast`, `--rp-duration-slow`, `--ease-out`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-icon-button-size` | `var(--spacing-control-md)` | inline and block size |
| `--rp-icon-button-radius` | `var(--radius-control)` | corner radius |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-icon-button[data-variant="primary"]` | `--_bg: var(--color-primary); --_fg: var(--color-primary-foreground)` |
| `.rp-icon-button[data-variant="secondary"]` | `--_bg: var(--color-secondary); --_fg: var(--color-secondary-foreground)` |
| `.rp-icon-button[data-variant="outline"]` | `--_border: var(--color-input)` |
| `.rp-icon-button[data-variant="link"]` | `--_fg: var(--color-link); --_hover: 100%; --_active: 100%` |
| `.rp-icon-button[data-variant="destructive"]` | `--_bg: var(--color-destructive); --_fg: var(--color-destructive-foreground)` |
| `.rp-icon-button[data-size="sm"]` | `--rp-icon-button-size: var(--spacing-control-sm)` |
| `.rp-icon-button[data-size="lg"]` | `--rp-icon-button-size: var(--spacing-control-lg)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-icon-button` | custom properties: the two public ones above; `--_bg: transparent; --_fg: var(--color-foreground); --_border: transparent; --_hover: 88%; --_active: 80%`. Declarations: `position: relative; display: inline-flex; flex-shrink: 0; align-items: center; justify-content: center; inline-size: var(--rp-icon-button-size); block-size: var(--rp-icon-button-size); margin: 0; padding: 0; border: 1px solid var(--_border); border-radius: var(--rp-icon-button-radius); background-color: var(--_bg); color: var(--_fg); font-family: inherit; font-size: var(--text-base); line-height: 1; text-decoration-line: none; cursor: pointer; user-select: none` |
| STATES | `.rp-icon-button:hover` | `background-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--_bg) var(--_hover), var(--_fg))` |
| STATES | `.rp-icon-button:active` | `background-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--_bg) var(--_active), var(--_fg))` |
| STATES | `.rp-icon-button:is(:disabled, [aria-disabled="true"])` | `opacity: 0.5; pointer-events: none` |
| STATES | `.rp-icon-button:is([data-pending], [aria-busy="true"])` | `color: transparent; pointer-events: none` |
| STATES | `.rp-icon-button:is([data-pending], [aria-busy="true"])::after` | `content: ""; position: absolute; inset: 0; margin: auto; inline-size: 1em; block-size: 1em; border: 2px solid var(--_fg); border-inline-end-color: transparent; border-radius: 50%` |
| MOTION | `.rp-icon-button` | `transition: background-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out), border-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out), color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out), opacity var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out), scale var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |
| MOTION | `.rp-icon-button:active` | `scale: 0.97` |
| MOTION | `.rp-icon-button:is([data-pending], [aria-busy="true"])::after` | `animation: rp-spin calc(var(--rp-duration-slow) * 2) linear infinite` |

The icon is `1.25em` of the root font size: 20 px. Sizes at default dials: `sm` 36 px, `md` 44 px, `lg` 52 px, square.

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function IconButton(props: IconButtonProps)`; generated on the Button pattern; renders `<Icon name={icon} />` as its only child; imports `Icon` from `./icon`.
- Remix: None; imports `Icon` from `./icon.tsx`.
- Liquid: `{% block 'icon-button', icon: 'close', aria_label: close_label, command_for: 'size-guide', command: 'close' %}{% endblock %}`; the block renders `{% render 'rp-icon', name: icon %}`. Params `form_id`, `dialog`, `tooltip` as Button. `{{ attributes }}` is printed LAST in the opening tag (D-035). Schema settings: `variant`, `size` (label keys of section 0.5). Locale keys: `ripple.icon-button.name`.

```tsx
<IconButton icon="close" ariaLabel="Close" commandFor="size-guide" command="close" />
```

```liquid
{% block 'icon-button', icon: 'close', aria_label: 'Close', command_for: 'size-guide', command: 'close' %}{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4 (RP104 reports a missing `icon` or `ariaLabel`; RP101 an unknown `icon`), plus RP201 (accessible name, `a11y.name.required: always`), RP301, RP302, RP114 (the targets of `commandfor`, `popovertarget`, `form`, `data-rp-open` and `aria-describedby` exist). Manifest `rules[]` (reported by RP113; the subject is the root, as for Button):

- `icon-button-needs-icon`: kind `requires-descendant`, selector `:scope`, target `svg.rp-icon`. Message: "An IconButton has no icon." Fix: "Pass the icon prop with one of the 24 icon names."
- `icon-button-single-icon`: kind `max-count`, selector `:scope > *`, count `1`. Message: "An IconButton has more than one child." Fix: "Keep only the icon; use Button for a control with text."
- `icon-button-needs-name`: kind `requires-attribute`, selector `:scope`, target `aria-label`. Message: "An IconButton has no accessible name." Fix: "Pass ariaLabel."
- `icon-button-command-pair`: kind `requires-attribute`, selector `button.rp-icon-button[commandfor]`, target `command`. Message: "An IconButton has commandfor without command." Fix: "Pass command together with commandFor."
- `icon-button-command-needs-target`: kind `requires-attribute`, selector `button.rp-icon-button[command]`, target `commandfor`. Message: "An IconButton has command without commandfor." Fix: "Pass commandFor together with command."
- `icon-button-dialog-needs-href`: kind `requires-attribute`, selector `button.rp-icon-button[data-rp-open]:not([commandfor])`, target `href`. Message: "An IconButton has the dialog prop but no href, so it does nothing without JavaScript." Fix: "Pass href with the page equivalent of the dialog, or open the dialog with commandFor and command."

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: `size` `sm`, `md`, `lg` give bounding boxes of 36, 44 and 52 px square; the icon inside is 20 by 20 px and centred (1 px tolerance).
3. Tier 5: fixture `default` computes a transparent `background-color` and `color` equal to the computed `--color-foreground`; fixture `link` computes `border-color` equal to the computed `--color-input`.
4. Tier 2: an IconButton called without `ariaLabel` fails the TypeScript build of the React and Remix conformance projects, and `ripple check` reports RP104 for the Liquid call.
5. Tier 5 and tier 9: fixture `dialog-closer` inside an open Dialog with id `size-guide` closes it on click and on Enter.
6. Tier 5: fixture `link-opener` in a page that contains an `rp-dialog` with `<dialog id="cart-drawer">`: after upgrade the link has `aria-haspopup="dialog"` and `aria-controls="cart-drawer"`; a click opens the dialog and the URL does not change. Tier 6: with JavaScript disabled a click navigates to `/cart`.
7. Tier 2: the canonical form of fixture `tooltip-trigger` equals the canonical form of the `<button>` inside `ui-tooltip-trigger` in fixture `default` of Tooltip (`10-components-interactive.md`).
8. Tier 2: an IconButton with `href="/cart"` and `tooltip="size-tip"` prints no `aria-describedby` and no `data-slot`; an IconButton with `dialog="cart-drawer"` and no `href` prints no `data-rp-open`.
9. Tier 1: each manifest rule of "Checker rules" has a failing sample in `components/icon-button/icon-button.test.ts` that produces exactly one RP113 finding with that rule id.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-031 (IconButton default `ghost`), D-068 as amended (IconButton REQUIRES `icon` and `ariaLabel`), D-008 item 2, D-009 as amended (`dialog`), D-082 (`tooltip`), section 6.4 (`formId`); `04-elements.md` section 10 examples (`rp-icon-button` closers); `ripple/docs/research/base-theme.md` (an icon button costs three statements).
- UNVERIFIED: as Button (SPIKE-05).

### Fallback (SPIKE-05)

Identical to the Button fallback: a `<button>` root with `commandFor` additionally prints `data-rp-open="<commandFor>"` (for `command="show-modal"`) or `data-rp-close="<commandFor>"` (for `command="close"`).

## Field

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `field` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `div` `.rp-field` |
| Liquid tier | `parts` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | yes |
| Depends on | `label`, `form-message` |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A wrapper that lays out one form control with its label, description and error message, and fixes the ids that link them: the control has `id="<id>"`, the label has `for="<id>"`, the description is `<id>-description`, the error is `<id>-error` (D-014, D-020).

- Use when: any form control needs a visible label, a hint or an error.
- Do not use when: the control has no visible label (pass `ariaLabel` to the control), or for a group of radios (use RadioGroup, which has its own legend).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Field` | `blocks/field.liquid` | `div` | `rp-field` | none | yes | the root prints NO `id` attribute; the `id` prop names the control |
| label | `FieldLabel` | `blocks/field-label.liquid` | `label` | `rp-label rp-field-label` | none | yes | `for="<field>"` |
| control | `FieldControl` | `blocks/field-control.liquid` | `div` | `rp-field-control` | none | yes | none; it contains the one control, which the caller gives `id="<field>"` |
| description | `FieldDescription` | `blocks/field-description.liquid` | `p` | `rp-field-description` | none | no | `id="<field>-description"` |
| error | `FieldError` | `blocks/field-error.liquid` | `p` | `rp-form-message rp-field-error` | none | no | `id="<field>-error"` |

Nesting rules:

1. All four parts are direct children of the root. Order for `orientation="vertical"`: label, control, description, error. Order for `orientation="horizontal"`: control, label, description, error. The binding does not reorder; the caller writes them in this order.
2. The control part contains exactly ONE form control component: Input, Textarea, Select, Checkbox, Switch, or QuantityInput (`10-components-interactive.md`).
3. The caller MUST wire the control itself, because a part cannot read its parent (`02-html-contract.md` S-7): pass `id="<id>"`; pass `describedBy` listing `<id>-description` when a FieldDescription is rendered and `<id>-error` when a FieldError is rendered (space-separated, description first); pass `invalid` when a FieldError is rendered.
4. A FieldError MUST be rendered only when the server has an error for the control. An empty FieldError MUST NOT be rendered.

### Props

Root (`Field`):

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `id` | id | the id of the control | none | yes | no | nothing (exception to FC-2: printing it would duplicate the control's id). It exists so that the checker and the fixture lint can verify that every part's `field` equals it |
| `orientation` | `orientation` | enum | `vertical horizontal` | `vertical` | no | no | `data-orientation` |

Label (`FieldLabel`), description (`FieldDescription`), error (`FieldError`):

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `field` | `field` | id | the root's `id` | none | yes | no | label: `for="<field>"`; description: `id="<field>-description"`; error: `id="<field>-error"` |

Control (`FieldControl`): no props other than `class` and rest props. Parts do not take `span` or `hidden`.

### Content model

`parts` - root: the four parts. Label: `text`. Control: exactly one form control. Description: `text`. Error: `text`.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (root `id="email"`; label, control with an Input):

```html
<div class="rp-field" data-orientation="vertical"><label class="rp-label rp-field-label" for="email">Email</label><div class="rp-field-control"><input class="rp-input" id="email" type="email" name="email" data-size="md"></div></div>
```

Fixture `invalid` (root `id="email"`; all four parts; the Input has `required`, `invalid`, `describedBy="email-description email-error"`):

```html
<div class="rp-field" data-orientation="vertical"><label class="rp-label rp-field-label" for="email">Email</label><div class="rp-field-control"><input class="rp-input" id="email" type="email" name="email" required data-size="md" aria-invalid="true" aria-describedby="email-description email-error"></div><p class="rp-field-description" id="email-description">We only use it for order updates.</p><p class="rp-form-message rp-field-error" id="email-error" data-tone="destructive" role="alert">Enter a valid email address.</p></div>
```

Fixture `horizontal` (root `id="gift"`, `orientation="horizontal"`; control with a Checkbox, then label):

```html
<div class="rp-field" data-orientation="horizontal"><div class="rp-field-control"><input class="rp-checkbox" type="checkbox" id="gift" name="gift" value="yes"></div><label class="rp-label rp-field-label" for="gift">This is a gift</label></div>
```

Fixture records (`parts` form):

| Fixture | Root `props` | `parts` (in order) |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{ "id": "email" }` | `label` `{ "field": "email" }` children `Email`; `control` children `<input class="rp-input" id="email" type="email" name="email" data-size="md">` |
| `invalid` | `{ "id": "email" }` | `label` `{ "field": "email" }` children `Email`; `control` children `<input class="rp-input" id="email" type="email" name="email" required data-size="md" aria-invalid="true" aria-describedby="email-description email-error">`; `description` `{ "field": "email" }` children `We only use it for order updates.`; `error` `{ "field": "email" }` children `Enter a valid email address.` |
| `horizontal` | `{ "id": "gift", "orientation": "horizontal" }` | `control` children `<input class="rp-checkbox" type="checkbox" id="gift" name="gift" value="yes">`; `label` `{ "field": "gift" }` children `This is a gift` |
| `class` | `{ "id": "email", "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | as `default` |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "id": "email", "orientation": "bogus" }` (output `data-orientation="vertical"`) | as `default` |
| `escaping` | `{ "id": "email" }` | `label` `{ "field": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` children `Email`; `control` children `<input class="rp-input" type="email" name="email" data-size="md" aria-label="Email">` (this fixture's lint skips checks `n-9` and `a-3`) |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `aria-invalid` | the control (not a Field part) | server, through the control's `invalid` prop | the server has an error | `true`, or absent |
| `aria-describedby` | the control | server, through the control's `describedBy` prop | a description or an error is rendered | the ids of the rendered parts, or absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None. Validation is native constraint validation; server-side errors arrive as a new page with a FieldError.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: label click focuses the control; native validation bubbles appear on submit; server errors are printed in FieldError.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<label for>` plus `aria-describedby`; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: the control is named by the FieldLabel. REQUIRED.
- Focus: clicking the label focuses the control (native).
- Announcements: the FieldError has `role="alert"`; it is announced when the page that contains it loads.
- `aria-describedby` MUST list only ids of elements that are rendered; `aria-invalid="false"` MUST NOT be printed (`02-html-contract.md` section 8).

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none (the control's own keys) |

### Styling

- File: `components/field/field.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--text-sm`, `--text-sm--line-height`, `--color-muted-foreground`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-field-gap` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 1.5)` | gap between parts |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-field[data-orientation="horizontal"]` | `--_columns: auto minmax(0, 1fr); --_column: 2; --_align: center; --_gap-x: calc(var(--spacing) * 2)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-field` | custom properties: `--rp-field-gap` as above; `--_columns: minmax(0, 1fr); --_column: 1; --_align: stretch; --_gap-x: 0`. Declarations: `display: grid; grid-template-columns: var(--_columns); align-items: var(--_align); row-gap: var(--rp-field-gap); column-gap: var(--_gap-x); min-inline-size: 0; margin: 0; padding: 0; border: 0` |
| PARTS | `.rp-field-label` | `min-inline-size: 0` |
| PARTS | `.rp-field-control` | `display: flex; align-items: center; min-inline-size: 0` |
| PARTS | `.rp-field-description` | `grid-column: var(--_column); margin: 0; font-size: var(--text-sm); line-height: var(--text-sm--line-height); color: var(--color-muted-foreground)` |
| PARTS | `.rp-field-error` | `grid-column: var(--_column)` |

In the horizontal orientation the control is placed in column 1 and the label in column 2 by grid auto-placement; description and error are forced into column 2.

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Field(props: FieldProps)`, `FieldLabel`, `FieldControl`, `FieldDescription`, `FieldError` in `field.tsx`; generated; no hooks and no React context (`02-html-contract.md` S-7). `FieldLabel` renders the Label contract with `htmlFor={field}` and the extra class; `FieldError` renders the FormMessage contract with `tone="destructive"`, the derived id and the extra class.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: five block files. `field-label` renders `{% render 'rp-label', html_for: field, class: 'rp-field-label', content: content %}`; `field-error` renders `{% render 'rp-form-message', id: error_id, tone: 'destructive', class: 'rp-field-error', content: content %}` where `error_id` is `field | append: '-error'`. Schema settings: `orientation` on `field`; none on the parts. Locale keys: `ripple.field.name`, `ripple.field.label_name`, `ripple.field.control_name`, `ripple.field.description_name`, `ripple.field.error_name`.

When a caller passes its own `class` to FieldLabel or FieldError, the output class is the embedded root class, then the part class, then the caller's classes: `rp-label rp-field-label custom`.

```tsx
<Field id="email">
  <FieldLabel field="email">Email</FieldLabel>
  <FieldControl>
    <Input id="email" type="email" name="email" required invalid describedBy="email-description email-error" />
  </FieldControl>
  <FieldDescription field="email">We only use it for order updates.</FieldDescription>
  <FieldError field="email">Enter a valid email address.</FieldError>
</Field>
```

```liquid
{% block 'field', id: 'email' %}
  {% block 'field-label', field: 'email' %}Email{% endblock %}
  {% block 'field-control' %}
    {% block 'input', id: 'email', type: 'email', name: 'email', required: true, invalid: true, described_by: 'email-description email-error' %}{% endblock %}
  {% endblock %}
  {% block 'field-description', field: 'email' %}We only use it for order updates.{% endblock %}
  {% block 'field-error', field: 'email' %}Enter a valid email address.{% endblock %}
{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4, plus RP202 (a control inside a Field with no associated label) and RP114 (duplicate or unresolved ids). RP104 at layer H MUST NOT require an `id` attribute on the `.rp-field` element (the id is on the control); at layer S it requires the `id` argument. Manifest `rules[]`, all RP113 errors:

1. `field-one-control`: `.rp-field` contains exactly one `.rp-field-control`, which contains exactly one of `input`, `select`, `textarea` that is not `type="hidden"`, and that control has an `id`.
2. `field-label-for`: `.rp-field` contains exactly one `label.rp-field-label` whose `for` equals the control's `id`.
3. `field-describedby`: when `.rp-field` contains `#<control id>-description` or `#<control id>-error`, the control's `aria-describedby` contains that id; every id in the control's `aria-describedby` resolves in the document.
4. `field-invalid`: the control has `aria-invalid="true"` if and only if `.rp-field` contains `.rp-field-error`.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: in fixture `invalid` the accessibility tree gives the input the name `Email` and the description `We only use it for order updates. Enter a valid email address.`; clicking the label focuses the input.
3. Tier 5: in fixture `horizontal` the checkbox and the label are on one row (their vertical centres differ by at most 2 px) and the checkbox is before the label in the inline direction, also with `dir="rtl"`.
4. Tier 1: each of the four manifest rules has a failing sample in `components/field/field.test.ts` that produces exactly one RP113 finding with that rule id.
5. Tier 6: with JavaScript disabled, submitting a form that contains fixture `invalid` with an empty value shows the browser's native "required" validation and does not navigate.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-014 as amended (parts `label`, `control`, `description`, `error`; the `control` part is a wrapper `div`, TSX export `FieldControl`; the CALLER passes `id`, `describedBy` and `invalid` to the control; Field's `id` is the control's id and is not printed on the Field root), D-020 (suffixes `-description`, `-error`; prop `field`); `02-html-contract.md` section 8 (Field row), S-7; `ripple/docs/research/uisdk-web-elements.md` sections 1 and 9.1 (`ui-field` rewrites attributes at upgrade; rejected).
- UNVERIFIED: None.

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.
