## Label

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `label` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `label` `.rp-label` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A native `<label>` for one form control.

- Use when: labelling a control outside a Field, or wrapping a control and its text.
- Do not use when: inside a Field (use FieldLabel, which prints this contract with the derived `for`), or for a group caption (use RadioGroupLegend).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Label` | `blocks/label.liquid` (markup in `snippets/rp-label.liquid`) | `label` | `rp-label` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: either `htmlFor` is set, or the label wraps exactly one form control. MUST NOT be inside `<a>` or `<button>`.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `htmlFor` | `html_for` | id | the id of a form control | none (omitted) | no | no | `for` |

### Content model

`text` - phrasing content; MAY contain one form control when `htmlFor` is unset.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (`htmlFor="email"`; children `Email`):

```html
<label class="rp-label" for="email">Email</label>
```

Fixture `wrapping` (no props; children a Checkbox then text):

```html
<label class="rp-label"><input class="rp-checkbox" type="checkbox" name="terms" value="1">I agree to the terms</label>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{ "htmlFor": "email" }` | `Email` |
| `wrapping` | `{}` | `<input class="rp-checkbox" type="checkbox" name="terms" value="1">I agree to the terms` |
| `class` | `{ "htmlFor": "email", "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | `Email` |
| `escaping` | `{ "htmlFor": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | `Email` |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

None.

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: clicking the label focuses or toggles its control (native).

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<label>`; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: it IS the name of its control.
- Focus: not focusable; activating it moves focus to the control.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/label/label.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--text-sm`, `--text-sm--line-height`, `--font-weight-medium`, `--spacing`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-label-font-size` | `var(--text-sm)` | font size |
| `--rp-label-font-weight` | `var(--font-weight-medium)` | font weight |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| none | |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-label` | `display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: calc(var(--spacing) * 2); margin: 0; padding: 0; font-size: var(--rp-label-font-size); line-height: var(--text-sm--line-height); font-weight: var(--rp-label-font-weight); cursor: default` |

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Label(props: LabelProps)`; generated; JSX prop `htmlFor`.
- Remix: prints `for` (lowercase attribute spelling).
- Liquid: `{% block 'label', html_for: 'email' %}Email{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: none. Locale keys: `ripple.label.name`.

```tsx
<Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
```

```liquid
{% block 'label', html_for: 'email' %}Email{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4, plus RP114 (the `for` target exists). Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: fixture `default` next to `<input id="email">`: clicking the label focuses the input; the input's accessible name is `Email`.
3. Tier 5: in fixture `wrapping`, clicking the text toggles the checkbox.
4. Tier 5: fixture `default` computes `font-size` 14 px and `font-weight: 500`.

## Input

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `input` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `input` `.rp-input` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A native single-line text input.

- Use when: collecting text, email, phone, URL, password, search terms, a number or a date.
- Do not use when: collecting a cart or product quantity (use QuantityInput), multi-line text (use Textarea), a choice (use Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Input` | `blocks/input.liquid` | `input` | `rp-input` | none | yes | inside a Field: `id` equals the Field's `id` |

Nesting rules: none. An Input MUST have a label: a Field, a Label, or `ariaLabel`.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `type` | `type` | enum | `text email tel url password search number date` | `text` | no | no | `type` |
| `name` | `name` | string | form field name | none (omitted) | no | no | `name` |
| `defaultValue` | `default_value` | string | initial value | none (omitted) | no | no | `value` |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `placeholder` |
| `autoComplete` | `auto_complete` | string | an `autocomplete` token list, for example `email`, `off`, `shipping postal-code` | none (omitted) | no | no | `autocomplete` |
| `min` | `min` | string | minimum (number or date) | none (omitted) | no | no | `min` |
| `max` | `max` | string | maximum (number or date) | none (omitted) | no | no | `max` |
| `step` | `step` | string | step | none (omitted) | no | no | `step` |
| `pattern` | `pattern` | string | a regular expression | none (omitted) | no | no | `pattern` |
| `required` | `required` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `required` |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `disabled` |
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md lg` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |
| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `aria-invalid="true"` |
| `describedBy` | `described_by` | string | space-separated ids | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-describedby` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-label` |
| `rpField` | `rp_field` | enum | `merchandise-id quantity line-id selling-plan note discount-code` | none (omitted) | no | no | `data-rp-field="<value>"`; an invalid value prints NO attribute (there is no default) |
| `formId` | `form_id` | id | id of a `<form>` in the same document | none (omitted) | no | no | `form` |

`min`, `max` and `step` accept a number in TSX; it is printed with `String(n)`.

`rpField` marks the control's meaning in a commerce form so that commerce elements do not depend on field names (D-043 as amended; `00-decisions.md` section 4.4). Callers MUST NOT pass `data-rp-field` through rest props or `attributes`. `formId` prints the native `form` attribute (the name `form` is reserved in Liquid, D-020). In reference HTML `form` is printed after the other native attributes and `data-rp-field` is printed last (FC-3).

### Content model

`none` - `<input>` is void.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props):

```html
<input class="rp-input" type="text" data-size="md">
```

Fixture `email-invalid` (`id="email"`, `type="email"`, `name="email"`, `defaultValue="ada@"`, `placeholder="you@example.com"`, `autoComplete="email"`, `required`, `invalid`, `describedBy="email-error"`):

```html
<input class="rp-input" id="email" type="email" name="email" value="ada@" placeholder="you@example.com" autocomplete="email" required data-size="md" aria-invalid="true" aria-describedby="email-error">
```

Fixture `search` (`type="search"`, `name="q"`, `size="lg"`, `ariaLabel="Search"`):

```html
<input class="rp-input" type="search" name="q" data-size="lg" aria-label="Search">
```

Fixture `commerce-field` (`name="discountCode"`, `rpField="discount-code"`, `formId="cart-lines-form"`, `ariaLabel="Discount code"`):

```html
<input class="rp-input" type="text" name="discountCode" form="cart-lines-form" data-size="md" aria-label="Discount code" data-rp-field="discount-code">
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | absent |
| `email-invalid` | `{ "id": "email", "type": "email", "name": "email", "defaultValue": "ada@", "placeholder": "you@example.com", "autoComplete": "email", "required": true, "invalid": true, "describedBy": "email-error" }` | absent |
| `search` | `{ "type": "search", "name": "q", "size": "lg", "ariaLabel": "Search" }` | absent |
| `number-disabled` | `{ "type": "number", "name": "amount", "min": "1", "max": "10", "step": "1", "pattern": "[0-9]+", "size": "sm", "disabled": true }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "type": "bogus" }` (output `type="text"`) | absent |
| `commerce-field` | `{ "name": "discountCode", "rpField": "discount-code", "formId": "cart-lines-form", "ariaLabel": "Discount code" }` | absent |
| `invalid-rp-field` | `{ "rpField": "bogus" }` (output equals fixture `default`: no `data-rp-field`) | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "name": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

The manifest `a11y.name` of Input, Textarea, Select, Checkbox and Switch is `label` ("receives its label from Field, Label or `ariaLabel`"); fixture lint check `a-2` skips the root of their fixtures (`02-html-contract.md` section 10, A-2).

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `value` | root | server | `defaultValue` is set | the value, or absent |
| `required`, `disabled` | root | server | the prop is true | present, or absent |
| `aria-invalid` | root | server | `invalid` is true | `true`, or absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None. (React hydration re-writes `type`, `value` and `name` with equal values; allowed by `REACT_HYDRATION_ALLOW`, `06-bindings.md` Q-10.)

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None. The input is uncontrolled in every host (D-036).

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: native editing, native constraint validation, submits with its form under `name`.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<input>`; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: from the associated `<label>` or `ariaLabel`; REQUIRED (RP202). `placeholder` is never the name.
- Focus: native. The focus ring comes from `base.css`.
- Announcements: None.
- The server MUST NOT print `aria-required` or `aria-disabled` (`02-html-contract.md` section 8).

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| Enter | submits the form (native) |

### Styling

- File: `components/input/input.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing-control-sm`, `--spacing-control-md`, `--spacing-control-lg`, `--spacing`, `--radius-field`, `--text-base`, `--color-input`, `--color-background`, `--color-foreground`, `--color-muted-foreground`, `--color-destructive`, `--rp-duration-fast`, `--ease-out`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-input-height` | `var(--spacing-control-md)` | minimum block size |
| `--rp-input-px` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 3)` | inline padding |
| `--rp-input-radius` | `var(--radius-field)` | corner radius |
| `--rp-input-font-size` | `var(--text-base)` | font size (16 px at every size, so that iOS does not zoom) |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-input[data-size="sm"]` | `--rp-input-height: var(--spacing-control-sm); --rp-input-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 2.5)` |
| `.rp-input[data-size="lg"]` | `--rp-input-height: var(--spacing-control-lg); --rp-input-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 4)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-input` | custom properties: the four public ones above; `--_bg: var(--color-background); --_fg: var(--color-foreground); --_border: var(--color-input)`. Declarations: `display: block; inline-size: 100%; min-inline-size: 0; min-block-size: var(--rp-input-height); margin: 0; padding-block: 0; padding-inline: var(--rp-input-px); border: 1px solid var(--_border); border-radius: var(--rp-input-radius); background-color: var(--_bg); color: var(--_fg); font-family: inherit; font-size: var(--rp-input-font-size); line-height: 1.25; appearance: none` |
| STATES | `.rp-input::placeholder` | `color: var(--color-muted-foreground); opacity: 1` |
| STATES | `.rp-input:hover` | `border-color: var(--color-foreground)` |
| STATES | `.rp-input:disabled` | `opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed` |
| STATES | `.rp-input:is([aria-invalid="true"], :user-invalid)` | `border-color: var(--color-destructive)` |
| MOTION | `.rp-input` | `transition: border-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Input(props: InputProps)`; generated; `defaultValue` is passed to React as `defaultValue`; JSX props `autoComplete`, `aria-invalid`, `aria-describedby`.
- Remix: prints `value` from `defaultValue` and `autocomplete` (lowercase).
- Liquid: `{% block 'input', id: 'email', type: 'email', name: 'email' %}{% endblock %}`. Params `rp_field`, `form_id`. The `rp_field` guard prints the attribute only when the value is in the list (no fallback value). `{{ attributes }}` is printed LAST in the opening tag (D-035). Schema settings: `size` (label keys of section 0.5). Locale keys: `ripple.input.name`.

```tsx
<Input type="search" name="q" ariaLabel="Search" />
```

```liquid
{% block 'input', type: 'search', name: 'q', aria_label: 'Search' %}{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none. An Input that holds a secret other than `type="password"` (a gift card code) SHOULD get `data-agent-secret` through rest props.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4 (RP101 reports a literal `rpField` outside its list), plus RP202 and RP114 (`aria-describedby` and `form` targets exist). Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: `size` `sm`, `md`, `lg` give heights 36, 44 and 52 px; every size computes `font-size` 16 px; in a 300 px parent the width is 300 px.
3. Tier 5: fixture `email-invalid` computes `border-color` equal to the computed `--color-destructive`; fixture `default` computes `border-color` equal to the computed `--color-input`.
4. Tier 2: fixture `default` prints no `value`, `name`, `required`, `disabled`, `form`, `data-rp-field` or `aria-*` attribute; fixture `invalid-rp-field` is canonically equal to fixture `default`.
5. Tier 6: fixture `email-invalid` inside `<form method="get" action="/x">` with the value `ada@example.com` submits `?email=ada%40example.com` with JavaScript disabled.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-014 as amended (item e), D-043 as amended (`rpField`), D-086, sections 4.4 (`data-rp-field`), 6.3 (`size` (controls)) and 6.4 (`formId`, `rpField`); `02-html-contract.md` section 8 (native controls row), PE-9; `06-bindings.md` Q-10.
- UNVERIFIED: None.

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.

## Textarea

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `textarea` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `textarea` `.rp-textarea` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A native multi-line text input.

- Use when: collecting a message, an order note, gift text.
- Do not use when: one line is enough (use Input).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Textarea` | `blocks/textarea.liquid` | `textarea` | `rp-textarea` | none | yes | inside a Field: `id` equals the Field's `id` |

Nesting rules: none. A Textarea MUST have a label: a Field, a Label, or `ariaLabel`.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `name` | string | form field name | none (omitted) | no | no | `name` |
| `defaultValue` | `default_value` | string | initial value | none | no | no | the TEXT CONTENT of the element, HTML-escaped (a `<textarea>` has no `value` attribute; exception to `02-html-contract.md` M-4 row 13) |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `placeholder` |
| `rows` | `rows` | number | a positive integer | `4` | no | no | `rows`; always printed |
| `required` | `required` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `required` |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `disabled` |
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md lg` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |
| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `aria-invalid="true"` |
| `describedBy` | `described_by` | string | space-separated ids | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-describedby` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-label` |
| `rpField` | `rp_field` | enum | `merchandise-id quantity line-id selling-plan note discount-code` | none (omitted) | no | no | `data-rp-field="<value>"`; an invalid value prints NO attribute (there is no default) |
| `formId` | `form_id` | id | id of a `<form>` in the same document | none (omitted) | no | no | `form` |

`rpField` and `formId` have the meaning stated under Input: `rpField` prints `data-rp-field` (D-043 as amended) and MUST NOT be replaced by rest props or `attributes`; `formId` prints the native `form` attribute. In reference HTML `form` is printed after the other native attributes and `data-rp-field` is printed last (FC-3).

### Content model

`none` - the caller passes no children; the text content is `defaultValue`. Bindings MUST print no whitespace between the start tag, the value and the end tag (whitespace inside `<textarea>` is content and is compared verbatim).

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props):

```html
<textarea class="rp-textarea" rows="4" data-size="md"></textarea>
```

Fixture `note` (`id="note"`, `name="note"`, `defaultValue="Leave at the door & ring <twice>"`, `placeholder="Order note"`, `rows=3`, `required`, `ariaLabel="Order note"`):

```html
<textarea class="rp-textarea" id="note" name="note" placeholder="Order note" rows="3" required data-size="md" aria-label="Order note">Leave at the door &amp; ring &lt;twice&gt;</textarea>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | absent |
| `note` | `{ "id": "note", "name": "note", "defaultValue": "Leave at the door & ring <twice>", "placeholder": "Order note", "rows": 3, "required": true, "ariaLabel": "Order note" }` | absent |
| `invalid-disabled` | `{ "name": "message", "size": "lg", "invalid": true, "describedBy": "message-error", "disabled": true }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "size": "bogus" }` (output `data-size="md"`) | absent |
| `commerce-field` | `{ "name": "note", "rpField": "note", "formId": "cart-lines-form", "ariaLabel": "Order note" }` (output `<textarea class="rp-textarea" name="note" rows="4" form="cart-lines-form" data-size="md" aria-label="Order note" data-rp-field="note"></textarea>`) | absent |
| `invalid-rp-field` | `{ "rpField": "bogus" }` (output equals fixture `default`: no `data-rp-field`) | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "name": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `required`, `disabled` | root | server | the prop is true | present, or absent |
| `aria-invalid` | root | server | `invalid` is true | `true`, or absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None. (React hydration re-sets the text of a `<textarea>` with an equal value; allowed by `REACT_HYDRATION_ALLOW`, `06-bindings.md` Q-10.)

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: native editing; submits with its form under `name`.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<textarea>`; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: from the associated `<label>` or `ariaLabel`; REQUIRED (RP202).
- Focus: native.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| Enter | inserts a line break (native) |

### Styling

- File: `components/textarea/textarea.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: as Input, without the control height tokens being used as a fixed height.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-textarea-min-height` | `calc(var(--spacing-control-md) * 2)` | minimum block size |
| `--rp-textarea-px` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 3)` | inline padding |
| `--rp-textarea-py` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 2.5)` | block padding |
| `--rp-textarea-radius` | `var(--radius-field)` | corner radius |
| `--rp-textarea-font-size` | `var(--text-base)` | font size |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-textarea[data-size="sm"]` | `--rp-textarea-min-height: calc(var(--spacing-control-sm) * 2); --rp-textarea-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 2.5); --rp-textarea-py: calc(var(--spacing) * 2)` |
| `.rp-textarea[data-size="lg"]` | `--rp-textarea-min-height: calc(var(--spacing-control-lg) * 2); --rp-textarea-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 4); --rp-textarea-py: calc(var(--spacing) * 3.5)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-textarea` | custom properties: the five public ones above; `--_bg: var(--color-background); --_fg: var(--color-foreground); --_border: var(--color-input)`. Declarations: `display: block; inline-size: 100%; min-inline-size: 0; min-block-size: var(--rp-textarea-min-height); margin: 0; padding-block: var(--rp-textarea-py); padding-inline: var(--rp-textarea-px); border: 1px solid var(--_border); border-radius: var(--rp-textarea-radius); background-color: var(--_bg); color: var(--_fg); font-family: inherit; font-size: var(--rp-textarea-font-size); line-height: var(--text-base--line-height); resize: vertical; appearance: none` |
| STATES | `.rp-textarea::placeholder` | `color: var(--color-muted-foreground); opacity: 1` |
| STATES | `.rp-textarea:hover` | `border-color: var(--color-foreground)` |
| STATES | `.rp-textarea:disabled` | `opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed` |
| STATES | `.rp-textarea:is([aria-invalid="true"], :user-invalid)` | `border-color: var(--color-destructive)` |
| MOTION | `.rp-textarea` | `transition: border-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Textarea(props: TextareaProps)`; generated; `defaultValue` is passed to React as `defaultValue` (React prints it as text content).
- Remix: prints the value as the single text child of `<textarea>`.
- Liquid: the block prints `>{{ default_value | escape }}</textarea>` with no whitespace. `{% block 'textarea', name: 'note', default_value: cart.note, aria_label: note_label %}{% endblock %}`. Params `rp_field`, `form_id` as Input. `{{ attributes }}` is printed LAST in the opening tag (D-035). Schema settings: `size` (label keys of section 0.5). Locale keys: `ripple.textarea.name`.

```tsx
<Textarea name="note" defaultValue={cart.note} ariaLabel="Order note" />
```

```liquid
{% block 'textarea', name: 'note', default_value: cart.note, aria_label: 'Order note' %}{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4 (RP101 reports a literal `rpField` outside its list), plus RP202 and RP114 (`aria-describedby` and `form` targets exist). Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 2: fixture `note` in all four hosts has a text content of exactly `Leave at the door & ring <twice>` (the element's `value` property) with no leading or trailing whitespace; fixture `default` has an empty `value`.
3. Tier 5: fixture `default` is at least 88 px high and 100% of its parent's width; it computes `resize: vertical`.
4. Tier 6: fixture `note` inside a GET form submits `note=Leave+at+the+door+%26+ring+%3Ctwice%3E` with JavaScript disabled.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-014, section 6.3; `02-html-contract.md` K-12 and its exception for verbatim text in `textarea` (accepted, D-075: `textarea` content is compared verbatim); `06-bindings.md` Q-10.
- UNVERIFIED: None.

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.

## Select

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `select` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `select` `.rp-select` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A native single-choice `<select>`. The options are plain `<option>` elements written by the caller.

- Use when: choosing one of more than five options, or a country, a sort order, a quantity from a list.
- Do not use when: there are five options or fewer and space allows (use RadioGroup), or the options need rich content (there is no custom listbox in V1).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Select` | `blocks/select.liquid` | `select` | `rp-select` | none | yes | inside a Field: `id` equals the Field's `id` |

Nesting rules: children MUST be `<option>` and `<optgroup>` elements only. `multiple` is not supported.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `name` | string | form field name | none (omitted) | no | no | `name` |
| `autoComplete` | `auto_complete` | string | an `autocomplete` token list | none (omitted) | no | no | `autocomplete` |
| `required` | `required` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `required` |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `disabled` |
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md lg` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |
| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `aria-invalid="true"` |
| `describedBy` | `described_by` | string | space-separated ids | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-describedby` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-label` |
| `rpField` | `rp_field` | enum | `merchandise-id quantity line-id selling-plan note discount-code` | none (omitted) | no | no | `data-rp-field="<value>"`; an invalid value prints NO attribute (there is no default) |
| `formId` | `form_id` | id | id of a `<form>` in the same document | none (omitted) | no | no | `form` |

`rpField` and `formId` have the meaning stated under Input: `rpField` prints `data-rp-field` (D-043 as amended) and MUST NOT be replaced by rest props or `attributes`; `formId` prints the native `form` attribute. In reference HTML `form` is printed after the other native attributes and `data-rp-field` is printed last (FC-3).
| `defaultValue` | (none; manifest `hosts: ["react"]`) | string | the `value` of the option selected initially | none | no | no | React only: passed to `<select defaultValue>`; React then prints `selected` on the matching `<option>`. In the `html`, `remix` and `liquid` hosts the caller writes `selected` on the `<option>` |

The initial selection is expressed in the contract by the `selected` attribute on one `<option>`. No host prints `aria-selected`.

### Content model

`flow` - restricted to `<option>` and `<optgroup>` elements.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props; children two options):

```html
<select class="rp-select" data-size="md"><option value="ca">Canada</option><option value="us">United States</option></select>
```

Fixture `sort` (`id="sort"`, `name="sort_by"`, `size="sm"`, `ariaLabel="Sort by"`; the second option selected):

```html
<select class="rp-select" id="sort" name="sort_by" data-size="sm" aria-label="Sort by"><option value="manual">Featured</option><option value="price-ascending" selected>Price, low to high</option></select>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` | `hosts` |
|---|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `<option value="ca">Canada</option><option value="us">United States</option>` | all |
| `sort` | `{ "id": "sort", "name": "sort_by", "size": "sm", "ariaLabel": "Sort by" }` | `<option value="manual">Featured</option><option value="price-ascending" selected>Price, low to high</option>` | `html`, `remix`, `liquid` |
| `sort-react` | `{ "id": "sort", "name": "sort_by", "size": "sm", "ariaLabel": "Sort by", "defaultValue": "price-ascending" }` | `<option value="manual">Featured</option><option value="price-ascending">Price, low to high</option>` | `react` (reference HTML is identical to `sort`) |
| `required-invalid` | `{ "name": "country", "autoComplete": "country", "required": true, "invalid": true, "describedBy": "country-error", "size": "lg" }` | `<option value="">Choose a country</option><option value="ca">Canada</option>` | all |
| `disabled` | `{ "name": "country", "disabled": true }` | `<option value="ca">Canada</option>` | all |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | as `default` | all |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "size": "bogus" }` (output `data-size="md"`) | as `default` | all |
| `commerce-field` | `{ "name": "sellingPlanId", "rpField": "selling-plan", "formId": "buy-form", "ariaLabel": "Delivery frequency" }` (output `<select class="rp-select" name="sellingPlanId" form="buy-form" data-size="md" aria-label="Delivery frequency" data-rp-field="selling-plan"><option value="">One-time purchase</option><option value="1">Every month</option></select>`) | `<option value="">One-time purchase</option><option value="1">Every month</option>` | all |
| `invalid-rp-field` | `{ "rpField": "bogus" }` (output equals fixture `default`: no `data-rp-field`) | as `default` | all |
| `escaping` | `{ "name": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | as `default` | all |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `selected` | one `<option>` child | caller (React: React, from `defaultValue`) | an option is selected initially | present on one option, or on none |
| `required`, `disabled` | root | server | the prop is true | present, or absent |
| `aria-invalid` | root | server | `invalid` is true | `true`, or absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None. A Select that must apply its value without a submit button is a commerce concern (SortSelect inside `rp-live`, `11-components-commerce.md`).

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: native picker; submits with its form under `name`.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<select>`; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: from the associated `<label>` or `ariaLabel`; REQUIRED (RP202).
- Focus: native.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| ArrowUp, ArrowDown | change the selected option (native) |
| Space, Enter | open the picker (native, platform dependent) |

### Styling

- File: `components/select/select.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: as Input.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-select-height` | `var(--spacing-control-md)` | minimum block size |
| `--rp-select-px` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 3)` | inline padding at the start |
| `--rp-select-radius` | `var(--radius-field)` | corner radius |
| `--rp-select-font-size` | `var(--text-base)` | font size |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-select[data-size="sm"]` | `--rp-select-height: var(--spacing-control-sm); --rp-select-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 2.5)` |
| `.rp-select[data-size="lg"]` | `--rp-select-height: var(--spacing-control-lg); --rp-select-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 4)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-select` | custom properties: the four public ones above; `--_bg: var(--color-background); --_fg: var(--color-foreground); --_border: var(--color-input); --_arrow-x: calc(100% - 1em); --_arrow-x2: calc(100% - 1em + 0.35em)`. Declarations: `display: block; inline-size: 100%; min-inline-size: 0; min-block-size: var(--rp-select-height); margin: 0; padding-block: 0; padding-inline-start: var(--rp-select-px); padding-inline-end: 2.5em; border: 1px solid var(--_border); border-radius: var(--rp-select-radius); background-color: var(--_bg); background-image: linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 50%, currentColor 50%), linear-gradient(135deg, currentColor 50%, transparent 50%); background-position: var(--_arrow-x) 50%, var(--_arrow-x2) 50%; background-size: 0.35em 0.35em; background-repeat: no-repeat; color: var(--_fg); font-family: inherit; font-size: var(--rp-select-font-size); line-height: 1.25; appearance: none; cursor: pointer` |
| STATES | `.rp-select:hover` | `border-color: var(--color-foreground)` |
| STATES | `.rp-select:disabled` | `opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed` |
| STATES | `.rp-select:is([aria-invalid="true"], :user-invalid)` | `border-color: var(--color-destructive)` |
| STATES | `.rp-select:dir(rtl)` | `--_arrow-x: 1em; --_arrow-x2: calc(1em - 0.35em)` |
| MOTION | `.rp-select` | `transition: border-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |

The chevron is drawn with two gradients in `currentColor`, so it follows the text colour and needs no image. `background-position` is a physical property with no logical form; the `:dir(rtl)` rule moves the chevron to the inline end in right-to-left (this rule sets custom properties in a STATES row; the CSS linter accepts `:dir(rtl)`, D-105 item 2).

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Select(props: SelectProps)`; generated; has the extra prop `defaultValue`. Writing `selected` on an `<option>` in React logs a development warning; use `defaultValue`.
- Remix: None; `selected` on `<option>` is printed unchanged.
- Liquid: `{% block 'select', name: 'country' %}<option value="ca">Canada</option>{% endblock %}`; the caller prints `selected` with `{% if %}`. Params `rp_field`, `form_id` as Input. `{{ attributes }}` is printed LAST in the opening tag (D-035). Schema settings: `size` (label keys of section 0.5). Locale keys: `ripple.select.name`.

```tsx
<Select name="sort_by" ariaLabel="Sort by" defaultValue="price-ascending">
  <option value="manual">Featured</option>
  <option value="price-ascending">Price, low to high</option>
</Select>
```

```liquid
{% block 'select', name: 'sort_by', aria_label: 'Sort by' %}
  {% for option in collection.sort_options %}
    <option value="{{ option.value | escape }}"{% if option.value == collection.sort_by %} selected{% endif %}>{{ option.name | escape }}</option>
  {% endfor %}
{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4 (RP101 reports a literal `rpField` outside its list), plus RP202 and RP114 (`aria-describedby` and `form` targets exist). Manifest `rules[]` (reported by RP113): `select-children`: kind `max-count`, selector `:scope > *:not(option):not(optgroup)`, count `0`. Message: "A Select has a child that is not option or optgroup." Fix: "Keep only option and optgroup elements inside Select."

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 2: fixtures `sort` (three hosts) and `sort-react` (React) produce canonically equal HTML, with `selected` on the second option.
3. Tier 5: `size` `sm`, `md`, `lg` give heights 36, 44 and 52 px; the element computes `appearance: none`; the chevron is visible (the computed `background-image` contains two gradients).
4. Tier 5: fixture `sort` has the `value` property `price-ascending` before any interaction.
5. Tier 6: fixture `sort` in a GET form submits `sort_by=price-ascending` with JavaScript disabled.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-014; `04-elements.md` section 10.10; `02-html-contract.md` section 3.4 (`selected` on `<option>`, written by the server).
- UNVERIFIED: Remix 3 printing the boolean attribute `selected` on `<option>` unchanged; decided by CA-4 on fixture `sort` (local test, no spike).

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.

## Checkbox

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `checkbox` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `input[type="checkbox"]` `.rp-checkbox` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A native checkbox.

- Use when: an independent yes/no choice that is applied when the form is submitted, or one of several choices in a filter list.
- Do not use when: the setting reads as on/off (use Switch), or exactly one of several options is chosen (use RadioGroup).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Checkbox` | `blocks/checkbox.liquid` | `input` | `rp-checkbox` | none | yes | inside a Field: `id` equals the Field's `id` |

Nesting rules: none. A Checkbox MUST have a label: a Field with `orientation="horizontal"`, a wrapping Label, or `ariaLabel`.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `name` | string | form field name | none (omitted) | no | no | `name` |
| `value` | `value` | string | value submitted when checked | none (omitted; the browser then submits `on`) | no | no | `value` |
| `defaultChecked` | `default_checked` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `checked` |
| `required` | `required` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `required` |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `disabled` |
| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `aria-invalid="true"` |
| `describedBy` | `described_by` | string | space-separated ids | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-describedby` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-label` |

`type="checkbox"` is always printed and is not a prop.

### Content model

`none` - `<input>` is void.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props):

```html
<input class="rp-checkbox" type="checkbox">
```

Fixture `checked` (`id="gift"`, `name="gift"`, `value="yes"`, `defaultChecked`):

```html
<input class="rp-checkbox" type="checkbox" id="gift" name="gift" value="yes" checked>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | absent |
| `checked` | `{ "id": "gift", "name": "gift", "value": "yes", "defaultChecked": true }` | absent |
| `required-invalid` | `{ "name": "terms", "value": "1", "required": true, "invalid": true, "describedBy": "terms-error", "ariaLabel": "Accept the terms" }` | absent |
| `disabled` | `{ "name": "gift", "disabled": true }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "name": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

Reference attribute order for this component: `class`, `type`, `id`, then the Props-table order (`type` is contract-fixed and printed right after `class`).

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `checked` | root | server | `defaultChecked` is true | present, or absent |
| `required`, `disabled` | root | server | the prop is true | present, or absent |
| `aria-invalid` | root | server | `invalid` is true | `true`, or absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None. (React hydration re-writes `type`, `value`, `name`, `checked` with equal values; `06-bindings.md` Q-10.)

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: native toggle; submits `name=value` when checked.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native checkbox; `checkPattern`: none. The server MUST NOT print `aria-checked`.
- Accessible name: from the associated `<label>` or `ariaLabel`; REQUIRED (RP202).
- Focus: native; the focus ring comes from `base.css`.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| Space | toggles (native) |

### Styling

- File: `components/checkbox/checkbox.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing-target-min`, `--radius-xs`, `--color-input`, `--color-background`, `--color-primary`, `--color-primary-foreground`, `--color-destructive`, `--rp-duration-fast`, `--ease-out`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-checkbox-size` | `var(--spacing-target-min)` | inline and block size (24 px: the minimum target) |
| `--rp-checkbox-radius` | `var(--radius-xs)` | corner radius |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| none | |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-checkbox` | custom properties: the two public ones above; `--_bg: var(--color-background); --_fg: var(--color-primary-foreground); --_border: var(--color-input)`. Declarations: `position: relative; display: inline-block; flex-shrink: 0; inline-size: var(--rp-checkbox-size); block-size: var(--rp-checkbox-size); margin: 0; padding: 0; border: 1px solid var(--_border); border-radius: var(--rp-checkbox-radius); background-color: var(--_bg); vertical-align: middle; appearance: none; cursor: pointer` |
| STATES | `.rp-checkbox:hover` | `border-color: var(--color-primary)` |
| STATES | `.rp-checkbox:checked` | `border-color: var(--color-primary); background-color: var(--color-primary)` |
| STATES | `.rp-checkbox:checked::after` | `content: ""; position: absolute; inset: 0; background-color: var(--_fg); clip-path: polygon(18% 52%, 27% 43%, 42% 58%, 73% 26%, 82% 35%, 42% 76%)` |
| STATES | `.rp-checkbox:disabled` | `opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed` |
| STATES | `.rp-checkbox:is([aria-invalid="true"], :user-invalid)` | `border-color: var(--color-destructive)` |
| MOTION | `.rp-checkbox` | `transition: background-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out), border-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |
| ENVIRONMENT | `.rp-checkbox` inside `@media (forced-colors: active)` | `appearance: auto` |

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Checkbox(props: CheckboxProps)`; generated; `defaultChecked` is passed to React as `defaultChecked`.
- Remix: prints `checked` from `defaultChecked`.
- Liquid: `{% block 'checkbox', id: 'gift', name: 'gift', value: 'yes' %}{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: none. Locale keys: `ripple.checkbox.name`.

```tsx
<Field id="gift" orientation="horizontal">
  <FieldControl><Checkbox id="gift" name="gift" value="yes" /></FieldControl>
  <FieldLabel field="gift">This is a gift</FieldLabel>
</Field>
```

```liquid
{% block 'field', id: 'gift', orientation: 'horizontal' %}
  {% block 'field-control' %}{% block 'checkbox', id: 'gift', name: 'gift', value: 'yes' %}{% endblock %}{% endblock %}
  {% block 'field-label', field: 'gift' %}This is a gift{% endblock %}
{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4, plus RP202 and RP114. Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: fixture `default` has a bounding box of 24 by 24 px; fixture `checked` computes `background-color` equal to the computed `--color-primary` and has a visible check mark (the `::after` pseudo-element computes `content: ""` and a non-`none` `clip-path`).
3. Tier 5: Space toggles the `checked` property; a click on an associated label toggles it.
4. Tier 2: no fixture has `aria-checked`.
5. Tier 6: fixture `checked` in a GET form submits `gift=yes` with JavaScript disabled.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-014; `04-elements.md` section 10.12; `ripple/docs/research/uisdk-web-elements.md` section 9.1 (`ui-checkbox` submits nothing without JavaScript); `02-html-contract.md` I-2.
- UNVERIFIED: `::after` on `input[type="checkbox"]` with `appearance: none` in WebKit and Gecko (SPIKE-06, tier 9). If it does not render, see the fallback.

### Fallback (SPIKE-06)

If a pseudo-element on a checkbox input does not render in WebKit or Gecko, the check mark is drawn without a pseudo-element: delete the `:checked::after` rule and add to the `:checked` rule `background-image: linear-gradient(var(--_fg), var(--_fg)); background-size: 50% 50%; background-position: center; background-repeat: no-repeat` (a filled square mark). The contract does not change.

## RadioGroup

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `radio-group` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `fieldset` `.rp-radio-group` |
| Liquid tier | `parts` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A `<fieldset>` of native radio buttons with a legend: exactly one option of the group is chosen.

- Use when: choosing one of two to five options that should all be visible.
- Do not use when: there are more than five options (use Select), or choosing a product variant (use VariantPicker, `11-components-commerce.md`).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `RadioGroup` | `blocks/radio-group.liquid` | `fieldset` | `rp-radio-group` | none | yes | none |
| legend | `RadioGroupLegend` | `blocks/radio-group-legend.liquid` | `legend` | `rp-radio-group-legend` | none | yes | none |
| item | `RadioGroupItem` | `blocks/radio-group-item.liquid` | `label` | `rp-radio-group-item` | none | yes (at least two) | none |
| input | none (printed by item) | (inside `radio-group-item`) | `input` | `rp-radio-group-input` | none | yes | none |
| label | none (printed by item) | (inside `radio-group-item`) | `span` | `rp-radio-group-label` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: the legend is the first child of the root; items follow. Every item of one group MUST receive the same `name` (a part cannot read its parent). At most one item has `defaultChecked`.

### Props

Root (`RadioGroup`):

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `orientation` | `orientation` | enum | `vertical horizontal` | `vertical` | no | no | `data-orientation` |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `disabled` on the `<fieldset>` (disables every radio in it natively) |
| `describedBy` | `described_by` | string | space-separated ids | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-describedby` |

Legend (`RadioGroupLegend`): no props other than `class` and rest props.

Item (`RadioGroupItem`):

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `name` | string | the group's field name | none | yes | no | `name` on the input |
| `value` | `value` | string | value submitted when chosen | none | yes | no | `value` on the input |
| `defaultChecked` | `default_checked` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `checked` on the input |
| `required` | `required` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `required` on the input |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `disabled` on the input |

On an item, `class`, `id` and rest props apply to the `<label>` (the item root).

### Content model

`parts` - root: one legend, then two or more items. Legend: `text`. Item: `text` (the option's label, placed in the label part).

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (legend `Size`; items `s` (checked) and `m`, `name="size"`):

```html
<fieldset class="rp-radio-group" data-orientation="vertical"><legend class="rp-radio-group-legend">Size</legend><label class="rp-radio-group-item"><input class="rp-radio-group-input" type="radio" name="size" value="s" checked><span class="rp-radio-group-label">Small</span></label><label class="rp-radio-group-item"><input class="rp-radio-group-input" type="radio" name="size" value="m"><span class="rp-radio-group-label">Medium</span></label></fieldset>
```

Fixture `horizontal-disabled` (root `orientation="horizontal"`, `disabled`, `describedBy="delivery-hint"`; second item `required`, `disabled`):

```html
<fieldset class="rp-radio-group" disabled data-orientation="horizontal" aria-describedby="delivery-hint"><legend class="rp-radio-group-legend">Delivery</legend><label class="rp-radio-group-item"><input class="rp-radio-group-input" type="radio" name="delivery" value="ship"><span class="rp-radio-group-label">Ship</span></label><label class="rp-radio-group-item"><input class="rp-radio-group-input" type="radio" name="delivery" value="pickup" required disabled><span class="rp-radio-group-label">Pick up</span></label></fieldset>
```

| Fixture | Root `props` | `parts` (in order) |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `legend` children `Size`; `item` `{ "name": "size", "value": "s", "defaultChecked": true }` children `Small`; `item` `{ "name": "size", "value": "m" }` children `Medium` |
| `horizontal-disabled` | `{ "orientation": "horizontal", "disabled": true, "describedBy": "delivery-hint" }` | `legend` children `Delivery`; `item` `{ "name": "delivery", "value": "ship" }` children `Ship`; `item` `{ "name": "delivery", "value": "pickup", "required": true, "disabled": true }` children `Pick up` |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | as `default` |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "orientation": "bogus" }` (output `data-orientation="vertical"`) | as `default` |
| `escaping` | `{ "describedBy": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | as `default` (this fixture's lint skips check `a-3`) |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `checked` | input of an item | server | the item's `defaultChecked` is true | present on at most one input |
| `disabled` | root | server | the root's `disabled` is true | present, or absent |
| `required`, `disabled` | input of an item | server | the item's prop is true | present, or absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: native radio group; arrow keys move the selection; submits `name=value` of the chosen item.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<fieldset>` + `<legend>` + radio inputs sharing `name`; `checkPattern`: none. No `role="radiogroup"` (`02-html-contract.md` PE-3) and no `aria-checked`.
- Accessible name: the group is named by the legend (REQUIRED); each radio by its wrapping `<label>`.
- Focus: Tab enters the group at the checked radio (or the first); native.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| ArrowDown, ArrowRight | select the next radio (native) |
| ArrowUp, ArrowLeft | select the previous radio (native) |
| Space | select the focused radio (native) |

### Styling

- File: `components/radio-group/radio-group.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--spacing-target-min`, `--text-sm`, `--text-sm--line-height`, `--text-base`, `--text-base--line-height`, `--font-weight-medium`, `--color-input`, `--color-background`, `--color-primary`, `--rp-duration-fast`, `--ease-out`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-radio-group-gap` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 2)` | gap between items |
| `--rp-radio-group-size` | `var(--spacing-target-min)` | size of the radio circle |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-radio-group[data-orientation="horizontal"]` | `--_direction: row; --_gap-x: calc(var(--spacing) * 6)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-radio-group` | custom properties: the two public ones above; `--_direction: column; --_gap-x: 0`. Declarations: `display: flex; flex-direction: var(--_direction); flex-wrap: wrap; row-gap: var(--rp-radio-group-gap); column-gap: var(--_gap-x); min-inline-size: 0; margin: 0; padding: 0; border: 0` |
| PARTS | `.rp-radio-group-legend` | `inline-size: 100%; margin-block-end: var(--rp-radio-group-gap); padding: 0; font-size: var(--text-sm); line-height: var(--text-sm--line-height); font-weight: var(--font-weight-medium)` |
| PARTS | `.rp-radio-group-item` | `display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: calc(var(--spacing) * 2); cursor: pointer` |
| PARTS | `.rp-radio-group-input` | `flex-shrink: 0; inline-size: var(--rp-radio-group-size); block-size: var(--rp-radio-group-size); margin: 0; border: 1px solid var(--color-input); border-radius: 9999px; background-color: var(--color-background); appearance: none; cursor: pointer` |
| PARTS | `.rp-radio-group-label` | `font-size: var(--text-base); line-height: var(--text-base--line-height)` |
| STATES | `.rp-radio-group-input:hover` | `border-color: var(--color-primary)` |
| STATES | `.rp-radio-group-input:checked` | `border-color: var(--color-primary); background-image: radial-gradient(circle, var(--color-primary) 0 40%, transparent 44%)` |
| STATES | `.rp-radio-group-input:disabled` | `opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed` |
| MOTION | `.rp-radio-group-input` | `transition: border-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |
| ENVIRONMENT | `.rp-radio-group-input` inside `@media (forced-colors: active)` | `appearance: auto` |

A `<legend>` inside a flex `<fieldset>` is laid out by the browser's fieldset algorithm, not as a flex item; `inline-size: 100%` keeps it on its own line in both orientations.

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `RadioGroup`, `RadioGroupLegend`, `RadioGroupItem` in `radio-group.tsx`; generated; `defaultChecked` is passed to React as `defaultChecked`; no context.
- Remix: prints `checked` from `defaultChecked`.
- Liquid: three block files. Schema settings: `orientation` on `radio-group`. Locale keys: `ripple.radio-group.name`, `ripple.radio-group.legend_name`, `ripple.radio-group.item_name`.

```tsx
<RadioGroup>
  <RadioGroupLegend>Size</RadioGroupLegend>
  <RadioGroupItem name="size" value="s" defaultChecked>Small</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem name="size" value="m">Medium</RadioGroupItem>
</RadioGroup>
```

```liquid
{% block 'radio-group' %}
  {% block 'radio-group-legend' %}Size{% endblock %}
  {% block 'radio-group-item', name: 'size', value: 's', default_checked: true %}Small{% endblock %}
  {% block 'radio-group-item', name: 'size', value: 'm' %}Medium{% endblock %}
{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4, plus RP115 (no hand-rolled `role="radiogroup"`). Manifest `rules[]`, RP113 errors: `radio-group-legend` (the first child of `.rp-radio-group` is `legend.rp-radio-group-legend`); `radio-group-one-name` (all `.rp-radio-group-input` elements inside one `.rp-radio-group` have the same `name`); `radio-group-one-checked` (at most one of them has `checked`).

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: in fixture `default` the accessibility tree has a group named `Size` with two radios named `Small` (checked) and `Medium`; ArrowDown from `Small` checks `Medium` and unchecks `Small`.
3. Tier 5: each radio circle is 24 by 24 px; the checked one shows the dot (computed `background-image` is not `none`).
4. Tier 5: in fixture `horizontal-disabled` the two items are on one row at 1280 px and every radio matches `:disabled`.
5. Tier 6: fixture `default` in a GET form submits `size=s` with JavaScript disabled.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-014 as amended (item d: part classes `rp-radio-group-item`, `rp-radio-group-input`, `rp-radio-group-label`; any `rp-radio` or `rp-label` class inside a RadioGroup in another document is a defect of that document, D-069), section 8.1 (`fieldset` + `input type="radio"`, tier `parts`); `04-elements.md` section 10.12; `02-html-contract.md` A-8 (groups in `<fieldset>` with `<legend>`).
- UNVERIFIED: None.

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.

## Switch

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `switch` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `input[type="checkbox"][role="switch"]` `.rp-switch` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A native checkbox presented as an on/off switch (`role="switch"`). It submits like a checkbox.

- Use when: a setting reads as on or off ("Subscribe and save", "In stock only").
- Do not use when: the choice is an agreement or one of a list (use Checkbox).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Switch` | `blocks/switch.liquid` | `input` | `rp-switch` | none | yes | inside a Field: `id` equals the Field's `id` |

Nesting rules: none. A Switch MUST have a label: a Field with `orientation="horizontal"`, a wrapping Label, or `ariaLabel`.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `name` | string | form field name | none (omitted) | no | no | `name` |
| `value` | `value` | string | value submitted when on | none (omitted; the browser then submits `on`) | no | no | `value` |
| `defaultChecked` | `default_checked` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `checked` |
| `required` | `required` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `required` |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean | `true false` | `false` | no | no | `disabled` |
| `describedBy` | `described_by` | string | space-separated ids | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-describedby` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none (omitted) | no | no | `aria-label` |

`type="checkbox"` and `role="switch"` are always printed and are not props.

### Content model

`none` - `<input>` is void.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props):

```html
<input class="rp-switch" type="checkbox" role="switch">
```

Fixture `on` (`id="subscribe"`, `name="subscribe"`, `value="1"`, `defaultChecked`):

```html
<input class="rp-switch" type="checkbox" id="subscribe" name="subscribe" value="1" checked role="switch">
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | absent |
| `on` | `{ "id": "subscribe", "name": "subscribe", "value": "1", "defaultChecked": true }` | absent |
| `disabled-labelled` | `{ "name": "stock", "required": true, "disabled": true, "describedBy": "stock-hint", "ariaLabel": "In stock only" }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "name": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `checked` | root | server | `defaultChecked` is true | present, or absent |
| `required`, `disabled` | root | server | the prop is true | present, or absent |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: native toggle; submits `name=value` when on.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native checkbox with `role="switch"` (the one `role="switch"` allowed by `02-html-contract.md` PE-3); `checkPattern`: none. The browser exposes the checked state; the server MUST NOT print `aria-checked`.
- Accessible name: from the associated `<label>` or `ariaLabel`; REQUIRED (RP202).
- Focus: native.
- Announcements: None.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| Space | toggles (native) |

### Styling

- File: `components/switch/switch.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--color-input`, `--color-background`, `--color-primary`, `--color-primary-foreground`, `--rp-duration-fast`, `--ease-out`, `--rp-dir`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-switch-width` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 11)` | inline size (44 px) |
| `--rp-switch-height` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 6)` | block size (24 px) |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| none | |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-switch` | custom properties: the two public ones above; `--_bg: var(--color-input); --_fg: var(--color-background); --_border: transparent; --_thumb: calc(var(--rp-switch-height) - 2px * 3); --_travel: 0`. Declarations: `position: relative; display: inline-block; flex-shrink: 0; inline-size: var(--rp-switch-width); block-size: var(--rp-switch-height); margin: 0; padding: 0; border: 1px solid var(--_border); border-radius: 9999px; background-color: var(--_bg); vertical-align: middle; appearance: none; cursor: pointer` |
| STATES | `.rp-switch::after` | `content: ""; position: absolute; inset-block-start: 2px; inset-inline-start: 2px; inline-size: var(--_thumb); block-size: var(--_thumb); border-radius: 9999px; background-color: var(--_fg); translate: calc(var(--_travel) * var(--rp-dir)) 0` |
| STATES | `.rp-switch:checked` | `--_travel: calc(var(--rp-switch-width) - var(--rp-switch-height)); background-color: var(--color-primary)` |
| STATES | `.rp-switch:checked::after` | `background-color: var(--color-primary-foreground)` |
| STATES | `.rp-switch:disabled` | `opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed` |
| MOTION | `.rp-switch` | `transition: background-color var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |
| MOTION | `.rp-switch::after` | `transition: translate var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |
| ENVIRONMENT | `.rp-switch` inside `@media (forced-colors: active)` | `appearance: auto; inline-size: var(--rp-switch-height)` |

The `:checked` rule sets the private variable `--_travel` in a STATES row: a state, not a prop, changes it, so it cannot be a MODIFIERS rule. Thumb size at default dials: 18 px; travel: 20 px, mirrored in right-to-left by `--rp-dir`.

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Switch(props: SwitchProps)`; generated.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'switch', id: 'subscribe', name: 'subscribe', value: '1' %}{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: none. Locale keys: `ripple.switch.name`.

```tsx
<Field id="subscribe" orientation="horizontal">
  <FieldControl><Switch id="subscribe" name="subscribe" value="1" /></FieldControl>
  <FieldLabel field="subscribe">Subscribe and save</FieldLabel>
</Field>
```

```liquid
{% block 'field', id: 'subscribe', orientation: 'horizontal' %}
  {% block 'field-control' %}{% block 'switch', id: 'subscribe', name: 'subscribe', value: '1' %}{% endblock %}{% endblock %}
  {% block 'field-label', field: 'subscribe' %}Subscribe and save{% endblock %}
{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4, plus RP202, RP114. RP115 MUST NOT flag `role="switch"` on `input[type="checkbox"].rp-switch`. Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: fixture `default` has a bounding box of 44 by 24 px; the accessibility tree role is `switch`, state unchecked; fixture `on` is checked and computes `background-color` equal to the computed `--color-primary`.
3. Tier 5: Space toggles the `checked` property; with `dir="rtl"` the thumb of fixture `on` is at the inline end (its bounding box left edge is within 3 px of the root's left edge).
4. Tier 6: fixture `on` in a GET form submits `subscribe=1` with JavaScript disabled.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-007 and D-014 (Switch is `<input type="checkbox" role="switch">`); `04-elements.md` section 10.12; `02-html-contract.md` PE-3.
- UNVERIFIED: `::after` on a checkbox input in WebKit and Gecko (SPIKE-06, tier 9).

### Fallback (SPIKE-06)

If a pseudo-element on a checkbox input does not render in WebKit or Gecko, the thumb is drawn with a background instead: delete both `::after` rules; add to the root rule `background-image: radial-gradient(circle closest-side, var(--_fg) 0 80%, transparent 84%); background-repeat: no-repeat; background-size: var(--rp-switch-height) 100%; background-position: var(--_position) 50%` with `--_position: 0%` in the root rule and `--_position: 100%` in the `:checked` rule, and add a STATES rule `.rp-switch:dir(rtl)` that swaps the two positions. The contract does not change.

## FormMessage

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `form-message` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `forms` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `p` `.rp-form-message` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A short message about a form or a control: an error, a success note or a hint, printed by the server in the response to a form post (`02-html-contract.md` PE-11). Commerce elements also fill an empty, hidden FormMessage with errors (D-045 item 4).

- Use when: reporting the outcome of a form submission next to the form, or an error for one control (through FieldError).
- Do not use when: the message concerns the whole page (use Banner), or is transient feedback for a script action (use `toast()`, `10-components-interactive.md`).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `FormMessage` | `blocks/form-message.liquid` (markup in `snippets/rp-form-message.liquid`) | `p` | `rp-form-message` | none (commerce components add `data-rp-part="error"` themselves) | yes | inside a Field: `<field>-error` (printed by FieldError) |

Nesting rules: none.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tone` | `tone` | enum | `destructive success info warning muted` | `destructive` | no | no | `data-tone`; and `role`: `alert` when the tone is `destructive`, `status` for every other tone |

### Content model

`text` - phrasing content.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props; children `Enter a valid email address.`):

```html
<p class="rp-form-message" data-tone="destructive" role="alert">Enter a valid email address.</p>
```

Fixture `success` (`tone="success"`, `id="newsletter-status"`; children `Thanks for subscribing.`):

```html
<p class="rp-form-message" id="newsletter-status" data-tone="success" role="status">Thanks for subscribing.</p>
```

The empty error element of commerce contracts is this contract plus three literal attributes that the commerce component prints itself (it is not a FormMessage fixture):

```html
<p class="rp-form-message" data-rp-part="error" data-tone="destructive" role="alert" hidden></p>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `Enter a valid email address.` |
| `success` | `{ "tone": "success", "id": "newsletter-status" }` | `Thanks for subscribing.` |
| `muted` | `{ "tone": "muted" }` | `At least 8 characters.` |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | `Enter a valid email address.` |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "tone": "bogus" }` (output equals `default`) | `Enter a valid email address.` |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `role` | root | server | always | `alert` for `tone="destructive"`, otherwise `status` |
| `hidden` | root (commerce error part only) | commerce element removes it when it fills the text | an error occurs | present (printed by the commerce component, not by FormMessage) |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None for FormMessage itself. (The commerce error part's text and `hidden` writes are on the allow-list of the owning commerce component, `05-commerce.md`.)

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: the server prints the message in the response page.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: live region; `checkPattern`: none. `role="alert"` (assertive) for errors, `role="status"` (polite) for everything else.
- Accessible name: not applicable.
- Focus: not focusable. After a failed form post the page SHOULD move focus to the first invalid control (the host's responsibility).
- Announcements: the message text, when the element is inserted or filled.
- The message MUST NOT rely on colour alone: the text states the problem.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/form-message/form-message.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--text-sm`, `--text-sm--line-height`, `--color-destructive`, `--color-success`, `--color-info`, `--color-warning`, `--color-muted-foreground`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| none | | |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-form-message[data-tone="success"]` | `--_fg: var(--color-success)` |
| `.rp-form-message[data-tone="info"]` | `--_fg: var(--color-info)` |
| `.rp-form-message[data-tone="warning"]` | `--_fg: var(--color-warning)` |
| `.rp-form-message[data-tone="muted"]` | `--_fg: var(--color-muted-foreground)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-form-message` | custom properties: `--_fg: var(--color-destructive)`. Declarations: `margin: 0; padding: 0; font-size: var(--text-sm); line-height: var(--text-sm--line-height); color: var(--_fg)` |

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function FormMessage(props: FormMessageProps)`; generated.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'form-message', tone: 'success' %}<children>{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: `tone`. Locale keys: `ripple.form-message.name`.

```tsx
{form.posted_successfully ? <FormMessage tone="success">Thanks for subscribing.</FormMessage> : null}
```

```liquid
{% if form.posted_successfully? %}
  {% block 'form-message', tone: 'success' %}{{ 'newsletter.success' | t }}{% endblock %}
{% endif %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4. Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 2: `tone="destructive"` prints `role="alert"`; each of `success`, `info`, `warning`, `muted` prints `role="status"`.
3. Tier 5: fixture `default` computes `color` equal to the computed `--color-destructive`, `font-size` 14 px, `margin` 0.
4. Tier 5: the commerce error markup above computes `display: none` while it has `hidden`, and renders like fixture `default` when `hidden` is removed and text is set.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-014, D-045 item 4 (exact error element), D-008 item 7; `02-html-contract.md` PE-11, A-8, A-11; `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 7.5 (tone-only components).
- UNVERIFIED: None.

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.
