## Badge

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `badge` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `feedback` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `span` `.rp-badge` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A short, non-interactive label attached to another object: "Sale", "New", "Sold out", a count.

- Use when: marking a product card, a price, a menu entry or a heading with a status of one to three words.
- Do not use when: the label is clickable (use Button with `size="sm"`), the message is a sentence (use Banner or Text), or the label states a cart count that changes without a page load (use CartCount, `11-components-commerce.md`).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Badge` | `blocks/badge.liquid` | `span` | `rp-badge` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: none. Badge is phrasing content.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `variant` | `variant` | enum | `solid soft outline` | `solid` | no | no | `data-variant` |
| `tone` | `tone` | enum | `neutral muted primary info success warning destructive sale` | `neutral` | no | no | `data-tone` |
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |

### Content model

`text` - phrasing content; MAY start with one Icon.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props; children `New`):

```html
<span class="rp-badge" data-variant="solid" data-tone="neutral" data-size="md">New</span>
```

Fixture `small` (`variant="soft"`, `tone="sale"`, `size="sm"`; children `Sale`):

```html
<span class="rp-badge" data-variant="soft" data-tone="sale" data-size="sm">Sale</span>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `New` |
| `small` | `{ "variant": "soft", "tone": "sale", "size": "sm" }` | `Sale` |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | `New` |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "variant": "bogus" }` (output equals `default`) | `New` |

In addition the generator MUST emit one fixture per pair of `variant` and `tone` (24 fixtures), named `<variant>-<tone>` (for example `outline-muted`), with props `{ "variant": "<variant>", "tone": "<tone>" }` and children `Label`. The contrast test below uses them.

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

None.

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: static markup.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: none; `checkPattern`: none. A Badge is plain text for assistive technology.
- Accessible name: not applicable. The text MUST carry the meaning by itself; tone is decoration.
- Focus: not focusable.
- Announcements: none.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/badge/badge.css` (layer `rp.components`). The complete, normative file is below and MUST be copied as written.
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--radius-control`, `--text-xs`, `--text-sm`, `--font-weight-medium`, `--color-foreground`, `--color-background`, `--color-muted`, `--color-muted-foreground`, `--color-primary`, `--color-primary-foreground`, `--color-info`, `--color-info-foreground`, `--color-success`, `--color-success-foreground`, `--color-warning`, `--color-warning-foreground`, `--color-destructive`, `--color-destructive-foreground`, `--color-sale`, `--color-sale-foreground`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-badge-height` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 6)` | minimum block size |
| `--rp-badge-px` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 2)` | inline padding |
| `--rp-badge-radius` | `var(--radius-control)` | corner radius |
| `--rp-badge-font-size` | `var(--text-xs)` | font size |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-badge[data-tone="<tone>"]` for the 7 non-default tones | `--_tone`, `--_on-tone`, `--_tone-text` (values of `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 7.5) |
| `.rp-badge[data-variant="soft"]` | `--_bg`, `--_fg` |
| `.rp-badge[data-variant="outline"]` | `--_bg`, `--_fg`, `--_border` |
| `.rp-badge[data-size="sm"]` | `--rp-badge-height: calc(var(--spacing) * 5); --rp-badge-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 1.5)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| all | all | as written in the file below |

```css
/* badge.css
   Component: Badge (id `badge`). Contract: 09-components-foundation.md, "Badge".
   Root:      span, class rp-badge
   Props:     data-variant = solid | soft | outline   (default solid)
              data-tone = neutral | muted | primary | info | success | warning | destructive | sale   (default neutral)
              data-size = sm | md   (default md)
   States:    none
   Public:    --rp-badge-height, --rp-badge-px, --rp-badge-radius, --rp-badge-font-size */
@layer rp.components {
  /* 1. ROOT */
  .rp-badge {
    /* 1a. public custom properties */
    --rp-badge-height: calc(var(--spacing) * 6);
    --rp-badge-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 2);
    --rp-badge-radius: var(--radius-control);
    --rp-badge-font-size: var(--text-xs);
    /* 1b. colour slots */
    --_bg: var(--_tone);
    --_fg: var(--_on-tone);
    --_border: transparent;
    /* 1c. other private custom properties */
    --_tone: var(--color-foreground);
    --_on-tone: var(--color-background);
    --_tone-text: var(--color-foreground);
    /* 1d. declarations */
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: calc(var(--spacing) * 1);
    box-sizing: border-box;
    min-block-size: var(--rp-badge-height);
    margin: 0;
    padding-block: 0;
    padding-inline: var(--rp-badge-px);
    border: 1px solid var(--_border);
    border-radius: var(--rp-badge-radius);
    background-color: var(--_bg);
    color: var(--_fg);
    font-size: var(--rp-badge-font-size);
    font-weight: var(--font-weight-medium);
    line-height: 1;
    white-space: nowrap;
    vertical-align: middle;
  }

  /* 2. PARTS */

  /* 3. MODIFIERS */
  .rp-badge[data-tone="muted"] {
    --_tone: var(--color-muted);
    --_on-tone: var(--color-muted-foreground);
    --_tone-text: var(--color-muted-foreground);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-tone="primary"] {
    --_tone: var(--color-primary);
    --_on-tone: var(--color-primary-foreground);
    --_tone-text: var(--color-foreground);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-tone="info"] {
    --_tone: var(--color-info);
    --_on-tone: var(--color-info-foreground);
    --_tone-text: var(--color-info);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-tone="success"] {
    --_tone: var(--color-success);
    --_on-tone: var(--color-success-foreground);
    --_tone-text: var(--color-success);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-tone="warning"] {
    --_tone: var(--color-warning);
    --_on-tone: var(--color-warning-foreground);
    --_tone-text: var(--color-warning);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-tone="destructive"] {
    --_tone: var(--color-destructive);
    --_on-tone: var(--color-destructive-foreground);
    --_tone-text: var(--color-destructive);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-tone="sale"] {
    --_tone: var(--color-sale);
    --_on-tone: var(--color-sale-foreground);
    --_tone-text: var(--color-sale);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-variant="soft"] {
    --_bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--_tone-text) 12%, transparent);
    --_fg: var(--_tone-text);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-variant="outline"] {
    --_bg: transparent;
    --_fg: var(--_tone-text);
    --_border: color-mix(in oklab, var(--_tone-text) 45%, transparent);
  }
  .rp-badge[data-size="sm"] {
    --rp-badge-height: calc(var(--spacing) * 5);
    --rp-badge-px: calc(var(--spacing) * 1.5);
  }

  /* 4. STATES */

  /* 5. MOTION */

  /* 6. ENVIRONMENT */
}
```

An Icon inside a Badge takes the Icon default size (`1em`), which is the badge font size.

- React: `export function Badge(props: BadgeProps)`; generated.

- Liquid: `{% block 'badge', tone: 'sale' %}Sale{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: `variant`, `tone`, `size`. Locale keys: `ripple.badge.name`.

```tsx

```

  {% block 'badge', tone: 'sale', variant: 'soft' %}{{ 'products.sale' | t }}{% endblock %}
{% endif %}

## Banner

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `banner` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `feedback` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `div` `.rp-banner` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | `icon` |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A prominent in-page message about the page or a section: an error summary after a form post, a shipping notice, a success confirmation. The server prints it; it does not appear or disappear by script.

- Use when: the message concerns a page, a form as a whole, or a section, and must stay visible.
- Do not use when: the message concerns one control (use FormMessage), is transient feedback for a script action (use `toast()`, `10-components-interactive.md`), or is the site-wide promotional bar (use AnnouncementBar, `12-components-chrome-and-blueprints.md`).

Banner has no dismiss button: dismissing needs JavaScript, and a Banner whose message must stay until the condition is resolved is not dismissible.

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Banner` | `blocks/banner.liquid` | `div` | `rp-banner` | none | yes | none |
| icon | none (printed by the root) | none | `svg` | `rp-icon rp-banner-icon` | none | yes (always printed) | none |
| content | none (printed by the root) | none | `div` | `rp-banner-content` | none | yes (always printed) | none |

Nesting rules: the root prints the icon part, then the content part; the caller's children go inside the content part. The icon and content parts are not separate exports.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `variant` | `variant` | enum | `soft solid outline` | `soft` | no | no | `data-variant` |
| `tone` | `tone` | enum | `info success warning destructive neutral` | `info` | no | no | `data-tone`; `role`: `alert` when the tone is `destructive`, `status` for every other tone; and the icon name of the table below |

| `tone` | Embedded Icon `name` | `role` |
|---|---|---|
| `info` | `info` | `status` |
| `success` | `check` | `status` |
| `warning` | `warning` | `status` |
| `destructive` | `warning` | `alert` |
| `neutral` | `info` | `status` |

### Content model

`flow` - Heading, Text, Link, Button, lists. The first Heading or Text states the message.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props; children `Free shipping on orders over $50.`):

```html
<div class="rp-banner" data-variant="soft" data-tone="info" role="status"><svg class="rp-icon rp-banner-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 11v5M12 8h.01"/></svg><div class="rp-banner-content">Free shipping on orders over $50.</div></div>
```

Fixture `destructive` (`tone="destructive"`, `id="form-errors"`; children `<p class="rp-text" data-variant="body" data-tone="neutral">Your cart could not be updated.</p>`):

```html
<div class="rp-banner" id="form-errors" data-variant="soft" data-tone="destructive" role="alert"><svg class="rp-icon rp-banner-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 4l9 16H3zM12 10v4M12 17h.01"/></svg><div class="rp-banner-content"><p class="rp-text" data-variant="body" data-tone="neutral">Your cart could not be updated.</p></div></div>
```

Fixture `success-solid` (`tone="success"`, `variant="solid"`; children `Thanks for your order.`):

```html
<div class="rp-banner" data-variant="solid" data-tone="success" role="status"><svg class="rp-icon rp-banner-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 12.5l4.5 4.5L19 7.5"/></svg><div class="rp-banner-content">Thanks for your order.</div></div>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `Free shipping on orders over $50.` |
| `destructive` | `{ "tone": "destructive", "id": "form-errors" }` | `<p class="rp-text" data-variant="body" data-tone="neutral">Your cart could not be updated.</p>` |
| `success-solid` | `{ "tone": "success", "variant": "solid" }` | `Thanks for your order.` |
| `warning-outline` | `{ "tone": "warning", "variant": "outline" }` | `Only 2 left in stock.` |
| `neutral` | `{ "tone": "neutral" }` | `We are closed on public holidays.` |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | `Free shipping on orders over $50.` |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "variant": "bogus" }` (output equals `default`) | `Free shipping on orders over $50.` |
| `invalid-tone-falls-back` | `{ "tone": "bogus" }` (output equals `default`: `data-tone="info"`, icon `info`, `role="status"`) | `Free shipping on orders over $50.` |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `role` | root | server | always | `alert` for `tone="destructive"`, otherwise `status` |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: the server prints the Banner in the page that needs it.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: live region; `checkPattern`: none. `role="alert"` for `destructive`, `role="status"` otherwise. A region that is already in the page at load is not announced by itself; after a failed form post the host SHOULD link the first invalid control from the Banner text.
- Accessible name: not applicable.
- Focus: not focusable. Links and Buttons inside it are in the normal tab order.
- Announcements: none by script.
- The icon is decorative (`aria-hidden="true"`). The text MUST state the kind of message ("Error:", "could not be") and MUST NOT rely on colour or icon.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/banner/banner.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--radius-surface`, `--text-sm`, `--text-sm--line-height`, `--text-lg`, `--color-foreground`, `--color-background`, `--color-info`, `--color-info-foreground`, `--color-success`, `--color-success-foreground`, `--color-warning`, `--color-warning-foreground`, `--color-destructive`, `--color-destructive-foreground`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-banner-padding` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 4)` | padding on all four sides |
| `--rp-banner-gap` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 3)` | gap between icon and content |
| `--rp-banner-radius` | `var(--radius-surface)` | corner radius |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-banner[data-tone="success"]` | `--_tone: var(--color-success); --_on-tone: var(--color-success-foreground); --_tone-text: var(--color-success)` |
| `.rp-banner[data-tone="warning"]` | `--_tone: var(--color-warning); --_on-tone: var(--color-warning-foreground); --_tone-text: var(--color-warning)` |
| `.rp-banner[data-tone="destructive"]` | `--_tone: var(--color-destructive); --_on-tone: var(--color-destructive-foreground); --_tone-text: var(--color-destructive)` |
| `.rp-banner[data-tone="neutral"]` | `--_tone: var(--color-foreground); --_on-tone: var(--color-background); --_tone-text: var(--color-foreground)` |
| `.rp-banner[data-variant="solid"]` | `--_bg: var(--_tone); --_fg: var(--_on-tone); --_border: transparent` |
| `.rp-banner[data-variant="outline"]` | `--_bg: transparent; --_fg: var(--_tone-text); --_border: color-mix(in oklab, var(--_tone-text) 45%, transparent)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-banner` | custom properties: the three public properties above; `--_bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--_tone-text) 12%, transparent)`; `--_fg: var(--_tone-text)`; `--_border: transparent`; `--_tone: var(--color-info)`; `--_on-tone: var(--color-info-foreground)`; `--_tone-text: var(--color-info)`. Declarations: `display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: var(--rp-banner-gap); box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: var(--rp-banner-padding); border: 1px solid var(--_border); border-radius: var(--rp-banner-radius); background-color: var(--_bg); color: var(--_fg); font-size: var(--text-sm); line-height: var(--text-sm--line-height)` |
| PARTS | `.rp-banner-icon` | `flex: none; inline-size: var(--text-lg); block-size: var(--text-lg); margin-block-start: calc((var(--text-sm--line-height) * 1em - var(--text-lg)) / 2)` |
| PARTS | `.rp-banner-content` | `flex: 1 1 0%; min-inline-size: 0` |

`--text-sm--line-height` is a unitless ratio in `tokens.css` (`03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 3.4), so `var(--text-sm--line-height) * 1em` is the line box height; the margin centres the icon on the first text line.

Text and Link components inside the content part keep their own colours. A caller that wants the message in the banner colour writes plain text or passes no `tone` to a `variant="plain"` Link.

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Banner(props: BannerProps)`; generated. The root renders `<Icon name={iconName} className="rp-banner-icon" />` (`iconName` is the icon chosen from the validated `tone`) then `<div className="rp-banner-content">{children}</div>`.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: the icon is `{% render 'rp-icon', name: icon_name, class: 'rp-banner-icon' %}` where `icon_name` is assigned with a `case` on the normalised `tone`. `{% block 'banner', tone: 'success' %}<children>{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: `variant`, `tone`. Locale keys: `ripple.banner.name`.

```tsx
{actionData?.error ? (
  <Banner tone="destructive" id="form-errors">
    {actionData.error}
  </Banner>
) : null}
```

```liquid
{% if form.errors %}
  {% block 'banner', tone: 'destructive', id: 'form-errors' %}{{ form.errors | default_errors }}{% endblock %}
{% endif %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4. Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 2: for each tone, the root `role` and the `d` attributes of the icon equal the table under Props and the icon set of section 0.6.
3. Tier 2: every fixture output contains exactly one `svg.rp-icon.rp-banner-icon` and exactly one `div.rp-banner-content`, in that order, as the only children of the root.
4. Tier 5: for each of the 15 pairs of `variant` and `tone`, in `data-scheme="light"` and `data-scheme="dark"`, the contrast ratio between the computed `color` of the root and its effective background is at least 4.5.
5. Tier 5: fixture `default` at 375 px viewport width: the content part's inline size plus the icon's inline size plus the gap equals the root's content-box inline size (0.5 px).

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

## Spinner

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `spinner` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `feedback` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `span` `.rp-spinner` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

An indeterminate activity indicator for a region whose content is loading.

- Use when: a region is being fetched by script and no layout placeholder fits (a streamed `Suspense` fallback, the body of a drawer before its content arrives).
- Do not use when: a Button is pending (Button draws its own spinner from `pending`; do not nest a Spinner in a Button), the shape of the coming content is known (use Skeleton), or progress is measurable (use Progress).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Spinner` | `blocks/spinner.liquid` | `span` | `rp-spinner` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: MUST NOT be placed inside Button or IconButton.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md lg` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | locale key `ripple.spinner.label` (`Loading`) | no | no | `aria-label` |

### Content model

`none` - the root is empty.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props):

```html
<span class="rp-spinner" data-size="md" role="status" aria-label="Loading"></span>
```

Fixture `large-labelled` (`size="lg"`, `ariaLabel="Loading cart"`):

```html
<span class="rp-spinner" data-size="lg" role="status" aria-label="Loading cart"></span>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | absent |
| `large-labelled` | `{ "size": "lg", "ariaLabel": "Loading cart" }` | absent |
| `small` | `{ "size": "sm" }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "size": "bogus" }` (output equals `default`) | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "ariaLabel": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `role` | root | server | always | `status` |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional as markup. A page MUST NOT print a Spinner for content that only script can replace unless the same region has a no-JS path (`02-html-contract.md` PE-1, PE-8); without script and without motion preference the ring is static.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: status; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: `aria-label`, default `Loading` (locale key `ripple.spinner.label`).
- Focus: not focusable.
- Announcements: none by script. When the Spinner is inserted by script the `role="status"` region is announced with its label.
- With `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` the ring does not rotate; it stays visible as a three-quarter ring.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/spinner/spinner.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--rp-duration-slow`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-spinner-size` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 6)` | inline and block size |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-spinner[data-size="sm"]` | `--rp-spinner-size: calc(var(--spacing) * 4)` |
| `.rp-spinner[data-size="lg"]` | `--rp-spinner-size: calc(var(--spacing) * 8)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-spinner` | custom properties: `--rp-spinner-size`. Declarations: `display: inline-block; box-sizing: border-box; inline-size: var(--rp-spinner-size); block-size: var(--rp-spinner-size); border: 2px solid currentColor; border-block-start-color: transparent; border-radius: 9999px; vertical-align: middle` |
| MOTION | `.rp-spinner` (inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`) | `animation: rp-spin calc(var(--rp-duration-slow) * 2) linear infinite` |

The ring takes the current text colour. It has no colour slots and no `tone`.

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Spinner(props: SpinnerProps)`; generated; `ariaLabel = "Loading"` is a default parameter value (`06-bindings.md` B-7).
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'spinner' %}{% endblock %}`; `aria_label` defaults to `{{ 'ripple.spinner.label' | t }}`. Schema settings: `size`. Locale keys: `ripple.spinner.name`, `ripple.spinner.label`.

```tsx
<Suspense fallback={<Spinner ariaLabel="Loading recommendations" />}>
  <Await resolve={recommendations}>{(r) => <Recommendations data={r} />}</Await>
</Suspense>
```

```liquid
{% block 'spinner', size: 'lg' %}{% endblock %}
```

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4. Manifest `rules[]` (reported by RP113, D-059): `spinner-not-in-button`: kind `forbids-ancestor`, selector `:scope`, target `.rp-button, .rp-icon-button`. Message: "A Spinner is nested inside a Button." Fix: "Use the Button pending prop instead of a nested Spinner."

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: fixtures `small`, `default`, `large-labelled` compute an inline size and block size of 16, 24 and 32 px at default dials.
3. Tier 5: with `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference` emulated, `getComputedStyle(root).animationName` is `rp-spin`; with `reduce` emulated it is `none`.
4. Tier 2: the Liquid host with no `aria_label` prints `aria-label="Loading"` using `locales/en.default.json`.
5. Tier 1: `<button class="rp-button" type="submit" data-variant="primary" data-size="md">` containing fixture `default` produces exactly one RP113 finding whose problem ends with `[spinner-not-in-button]`; fixture `default` alone produces none.

## Skeleton

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `skeleton` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `feedback` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `div` `.rp-skeleton` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A grey block that reserves the box of content that script will load, so that the layout does not shift when the content arrives.

- Use when: writing the fallback of a streamed or script-loaded region whose shape is known (a recommendations row, a predictive-search result list).
- Do not use when: the server already has the data (print the data), an image position has no image (use Placeholder), or the wait has no known shape (use Spinner).

A Skeleton MUST NOT be the only content of a region in the no-JS page: a region that script fills MUST have a server-rendered no-JS path, or be omitted without script (`02-html-contract.md` PE-1, PE-8).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Skeleton` | `blocks/skeleton.liquid` | `div` | `rp-skeleton` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: none.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ratio` | `ratio` | enum | `auto 1/1 4/5 3/4 2/3 4/3 3/2 16/9 21/9` | `auto` | no | no | `data-ratio` |
| `radius` | `radius` | enum | `none sm md lg xl full` | `md` | no | no | `data-radius` |

`ratio="auto"` is a text line: block size `1em` of the surrounding font size. Any other ratio is a media box: full inline size, block size from the ratio. Inline size is set with the public custom property `--rp-skeleton-width` (for example `class="[--rp-skeleton-width:60%]"` with Tailwind, or a wrapping Grid cell).

### Content model

`none` - the root is empty.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props):

```html
<div class="rp-skeleton" data-ratio="auto" data-radius="md" aria-hidden="true"></div>
```

Fixture `media` (`ratio="4/5"`, `radius="lg"`):

```html
<div class="rp-skeleton" data-ratio="4/5" data-radius="lg" aria-hidden="true"></div>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | absent |
| `media` | `{ "ratio": "4/5", "radius": "lg" }` | absent |
| `avatar` | `{ "ratio": "1/1", "radius": "full" }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "ratio": "bogus" }` (output equals `default`) | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `aria-hidden` | root | server | always | `true` |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional as markup: a static grey block (the pulse runs without script because it is CSS).

None.

- Accessible name: not applicable. The region that contains Skeletons SHOULD carry `aria-busy="true"` while it loads; that attribute is the caller's.
- Focus: not focusable.
- Announcements: none.
- With `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` the block does not pulse.

| none | none |

- Tokens used: `--color-muted`, `--radius-sm`, `--radius-md`, `--radius-lg`, `--radius-xl`, `--rp-duration-slow`, `--ease-in-out`.

| ROOT | `.rp-skeleton` | custom properties: `--rp-skeleton-width: 100%`; `--_ratio: auto`; `--_block-size: 1em`; `--_radius: var(--radius-md)`. Declarations: `display: block; box-sizing: border-box; inline-size: var(--rp-skeleton-width); block-size: var(--_block-size); aspect-ratio: var(--_ratio); margin: 0; border-radius: var(--_radius); background-color: var(--color-muted)` |
| MOTION | `.rp-skeleton` (inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`) | `animation: rp-pulse calc(var(--rp-duration-slow) * 5) var(--ease-in-out) infinite` |
| ENVIRONMENT | `.rp-skeleton` (inside `@media (forced-colors: active)`) | `border: 1px solid CanvasText` |

## Progress

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `progress` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `feedback` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `progress` `.rp-progress` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A horizontal bar that shows how much of a measurable quantity is complete, on the native `<progress>` element.

- Use when: showing progress toward a threshold the server can compute (amount left until free shipping, step 2 of 4, stock remaining).
- Do not use when: the quantity is not measurable (use Spinner), or the value is a rating (use Rating).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Progress` | `blocks/progress.liquid` | `progress` | `rp-progress` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: none. The bar has no visible text; the caller prints the sentence that explains it ("$12.00 away from free shipping") next to it as Text.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `value` | `value` | number | `0` to `max` | none (omitted) | no | no | `value`. Unset means indeterminate: no `value` attribute |
| `max` | `max` | number | greater than `0` | `100` | no | no | `max` (always printed) |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text | none | yes | no | `aria-label` |
| `tone` | `tone` | enum | `primary success warning destructive` | `primary` | no | no | `data-tone` |
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |

`value={0}` is set (it prints `value="0"`); only `undefined`, `null`, Liquid `nil` and the empty string are unset (`02-html-contract.md` M-5). Hosts MUST NOT clamp `value`; the browser clamps it to `max`.

### Content model

`none` - the element is printed with no fallback content.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (`value={40}`, `ariaLabel="Progress to free shipping"`):

```html
<progress class="rp-progress" value="40" max="100" data-tone="primary" data-size="md" aria-label="Progress to free shipping"></progress>
```

Fixture `indeterminate` (`ariaLabel="Loading"`):

```html
<progress class="rp-progress" max="100" data-tone="primary" data-size="md" aria-label="Loading"></progress>
```

Fixture `steps` (`value={2}`, `max={4}`, `tone="success"`, `size="sm"`, `ariaLabel="Step 2 of 4"`):

```html
<progress class="rp-progress" value="2" max="4" data-tone="success" data-size="sm" aria-label="Step 2 of 4"></progress>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{ "value": 40, "ariaLabel": "Progress to free shipping" }` | absent |
| `indeterminate` | `{ "ariaLabel": "Loading" }` | absent |
| `steps` | `{ "value": 2, "max": 4, "tone": "success", "size": "sm", "ariaLabel": "Step 2 of 4" }` | absent |
| `zero` | `{ "value": 0, "ariaLabel": "Progress to free shipping" }` (output has `value="0"`) | absent |
| `class` | `{ "value": 40, "ariaLabel": "Progress to free shipping", "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "value": 40, "ariaLabel": "Progress to free shipping", "tone": "bogus" }` (output equals `default`) | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "value": 40, "ariaLabel": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

None.

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None. (A commerce element that updates a Progress, for example a free-shipping bar inside the cart drawer, replaces the server-rendered fragment that contains it; it does not write `value`. See `05-commerce.md`.)

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: the server prints `value` and `max`.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: native `<progress>` (implicit role `progressbar`); `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: `aria-label` (required). When a visible Text explains the bar, the label SHOULD repeat that sentence.
- Focus: not focusable.
- Announcements: none. A caller that needs the new amount announced after a cart change uses the commerce announcement of `05-commerce.md`.
- In forced-colours mode the track has a 1 px `CanvasText` border and the value bar is `Highlight`.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/progress/progress.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--color-muted`, `--color-primary`, `--color-success`, `--color-warning`, `--color-destructive`, `--rp-duration-base`, `--rp-duration-slow`, `--ease-out`, `--ease-in-out`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-progress-height` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 2)` | block size of the bar |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-progress[data-tone="success"]` | `--_fg: var(--color-success)` |
| `.rp-progress[data-tone="warning"]` | `--_fg: var(--color-warning)` |
| `.rp-progress[data-tone="destructive"]` | `--_fg: var(--color-destructive)` |
| `.rp-progress[data-size="sm"]` | `--rp-progress-height: calc(var(--spacing) * 1)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-progress` | custom properties: `--rp-progress-height`; `--_bg: var(--color-muted)`; `--_fg: var(--color-primary)`; `--_border: transparent`. Declarations: `appearance: none; display: block; box-sizing: border-box; inline-size: 100%; block-size: var(--rp-progress-height); margin: 0; padding: 0; overflow: hidden; border: 0; border-radius: 9999px; background-color: var(--_bg); color: var(--_fg)` |
| STATES | `.rp-progress::-webkit-progress-bar` | `background-color: transparent` |
| STATES | `.rp-progress::-webkit-progress-value` | `border-radius: 9999px; background-color: var(--_fg)` |
| STATES | `.rp-progress::-moz-progress-bar` | `border-radius: 9999px; background-color: var(--_fg)` |
| STATES | `.rp-progress:indeterminate` | `background-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--_fg) 40%, var(--_bg))` |
| STATES | `.rp-progress:indeterminate::-moz-progress-bar` | `background-color: transparent` |
| MOTION | `.rp-progress::-webkit-progress-value` (inside the motion media query) | `transition: inline-size var(--rp-duration-base) var(--ease-out)` |
| MOTION | `.rp-progress:indeterminate` (inside the motion media query) | `animation: rp-pulse calc(var(--rp-duration-slow) * 5) var(--ease-in-out) infinite` |
| ENVIRONMENT | `.rp-progress` (inside `@media (forced-colors: active)`) | `border: 1px solid CanvasText` |
| ENVIRONMENT | `.rp-progress::-webkit-progress-value` and, as a SEPARATE rule, `.rp-progress::-moz-progress-bar` (inside `@media (forced-colors: active)`) | `background-color: Highlight` |

Rules for this file:

1. The three vendor pseudo-element selectors MUST each be a rule of their own. They MUST NOT share a selector list: a browser drops the whole rule when one selector in the list is unknown to it.
2. `--_border` is declared because CSS-08 requires the three colour slots by name; the root consumes it only in forced colours (the track is borderless otherwise, which is the exception to CSS-26 for a non-control).
3. The `transition` on `inline-size` is the single exception in this document to `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 8 rule 3 (property list). It applies only where a script replaces the element in place with a preserved node, and is harmless otherwise. The CSS linter accepts this one `transition` on `inline-size` (D-105 item 2).

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Progress(props: ProgressProps)`; generated; `value?: number`, `max?: number`.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'progress', value: cart.total_price, max: threshold, aria_label: label %}{% endblock %}`. Numbers are passed as numbers, not strings. Schema settings: `tone`, `size`. Locale keys: `ripple.progress.name`.

```tsx
<Progress value={cart.cost.subtotalAmount.amount} max={50} ariaLabel="Progress to free shipping" />
```

```liquid
{% assign threshold = 5000 %}
{% block 'progress', value: cart.total_price, max: threshold, aria_label: 'Progress to free shipping' %}{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4 (RP104 reports a missing `ariaLabel`). Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 2: fixture `indeterminate` has no `value` attribute; fixture `zero` has `value="0"`; every fixture has a `max` attribute.
3. Tier 5: fixture `default` in a 200 px wide container computes inline size 200 px and block size 8 px; fixture `steps` computes block size 4 px; `document.querySelector("progress").position` is `0.4` for `default`, `0.5` for `steps` and `-1` for `indeterminate`.
4. Tier 5 (Chromium, Firefox, WebKit): a screenshot of fixture `default` in a 200 px container shows the value colour (computed `--color-primary`) at x = 40 px and the track colour (computed `--color-muted`) at x = 160 px, sampled on the vertical centre line.
5. Tier 3: the accessibility snapshot of fixture `default` has role `progressbar` and name `Progress to free shipping`.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` section 8.1 (Progress), section 6.3 (`tone`, `size`); `ripple/docs/03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 7.2 (STATES selector forms), CSS-12, section 8.
- Accepted by the CSS linter (D-105 item 2): `::-webkit-progress-bar`, `::-webkit-progress-value`, `::-moz-progress-bar`, `:indeterminate`, and one `transition` on `inline-size`.
- UNVERIFIED: that Firefox draws no `::-moz-progress-bar` fill for an indeterminate bar with `appearance: none`; the `:indeterminate::-moz-progress-bar` rule makes the result the same either way.

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.

## EmptyState

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `empty-state` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `feedback` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `div` `.rp-empty-state` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | `icon` |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A centred block that explains that a list or region has nothing in it and offers the next step: an empty cart, a search with no results, an empty collection.

- Use when: a region that normally lists items has none.
- Do not use when: the region failed to load (use Banner with `tone="destructive"`), or the page does not exist (the 404 page is a blueprint, `12-components-chrome-and-blueprints.md`, which uses EmptyState inside it).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `EmptyState` | `blocks/empty-state.liquid` | `div` | `rp-empty-state` | none | yes | none |
| icon | none (printed by the root when `icon` is set) | none | `svg` | `rp-icon rp-empty-state-icon` | none | no | none |

Nesting rules: children SHOULD be, in order: one Heading, at most one Text, at most two Buttons or Links. The Heading level is the caller's choice and MUST fit the page outline.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `icon` | `icon` | enum | the 24 icon names of section 0.6 | none (omitted) | no | no | when set to a known name: the embedded Icon with class `rp-icon rp-empty-state-icon` as the first child. Unset or unknown: nothing |

### Content model

`flow` - Heading, Text, Button, Link.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props; children `<h2 class="rp-heading">Your cart is empty</h2>`):

```html
<div class="rp-empty-state"><h2 class="rp-heading">Your cart is empty</h2></div>
```

Fixture `with-icon-and-action` (`icon="cart"`; children: Heading, Text, Button link):

```html
<div class="rp-empty-state"><svg class="rp-icon rp-empty-state-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 4h2l2.4 11h10.2L20 7H6.2"/><circle cx="9" cy="19" r="1.5"/><circle cx="17" cy="19" r="1.5"/></svg><h2 class="rp-heading">Your cart is empty</h2><p class="rp-text" data-variant="body" data-tone="neutral">Items you add appear here.</p><a class="rp-button" href="/collections/all" data-variant="primary" data-size="md">Continue shopping</a></div>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `<h2 class="rp-heading">Your cart is empty</h2>` |
| `with-icon-and-action` | `{ "icon": "cart" }` | `<h2 class="rp-heading">Your cart is empty</h2><p class="rp-text" data-variant="body" data-tone="neutral">Items you add appear here.</p><a class="rp-button" href="/collections/all" data-variant="primary" data-size="md">Continue shopping</a>` |
| `unknown-icon` | `{ "icon": "bogus" }` (output equals `default`) | `<h2 class="rp-heading">Your cart is empty</h2>` |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | `<h2 class="rp-heading">Your cart is empty</h2>` |

`invalid-enum-falls-back` does not apply (`icon` has no default); `unknown-icon` takes its place.

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

None.

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: static markup; the action is a link or a form button.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: none; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: not applicable. The Heading names the state.
- Focus: not focusable; the actions are in the normal tab order.
- Announcements: none. When a commerce element replaces a list with an EmptyState (the last cart line is removed) the owning element announces the change (`05-commerce.md`).
- The icon is decorative (`aria-hidden="true"`).

| Modifier selector | Sets |

| none | |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-empty-state` | custom properties: the three public properties above. Declarations: `display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: var(--rp-empty-state-gap); box-sizing: border-box; max-inline-size: var(--container-prose); margin-block: 0; margin-inline: auto; padding-block: var(--rp-empty-state-padding); padding-inline: 0; text-align: center` |

## Rating

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `rating` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `feedback` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `span` `.rp-rating` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

A read-only display of an average rating as five stars filled in half-star steps, with an optional review count after it.

- Use when: showing a product's average review score on a card or a product page.
- Do not use when: the shopper sets a rating (that is a RadioGroup with five options inside a review form; Ripple has no rating input), or the product has no reviews (print nothing).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Rating` | `blocks/rating.liquid` | `span` | `rp-rating` | none | yes | none |
| stars | none (printed by the root) | none | `span` | `rp-rating-stars` | none | yes (always printed) | none |

Nesting rules: none. Rating is phrasing content.

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `value` | `value` | enum | `0 0.5 1 1.5 2 2.5 3 3.5 4 4.5 5` | `0` | no | no | `data-value` on the root |
| `size` | `size` | enum | `sm md lg` | `md` | no | no | `data-size` |
| `ariaLabel` | `aria_label` | string | text, for example `4.5 out of 5 stars` | none | yes | no | `aria-label` on the stars part |

`value` is an enumeration, not a number, because the contract template has no arithmetic and the fill is selected by CSS attribute selectors. The caller rounds to the nearest half before passing it:

- TSX: `value` accepts the number or the string; `String(Math.round(n * 2) / 2)` gives a member of the list for `0 <= n <= 5`.
- Liquid: `{% assign v = n | times: 2 | round | divided_by: 2.0 | remove: '.0' %}` (`4.0` becomes `4`, `4.5` stays `4.5`).

A value outside the list prints `data-value="0"` (FC-6).

### Content model

`text` - optional phrasing content printed after the stars: the review count, for example `(128)`. MAY be empty.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (`ariaLabel="0 out of 5 stars"`; no children):

```html
<span class="rp-rating" data-value="0" data-size="md"><span class="rp-rating-stars" role="img" aria-label="0 out of 5 stars"></span></span>
```

Fixture `with-count` (`value="4.5"`, `ariaLabel="4.5 out of 5 stars"`; children `(128)`):

```html
<span class="rp-rating" data-value="4.5" data-size="md"><span class="rp-rating-stars" role="img" aria-label="4.5 out of 5 stars"></span>(128)</span>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{ "ariaLabel": "0 out of 5 stars" }` | absent |
| `with-count` | `{ "value": "4.5", "ariaLabel": "4.5 out of 5 stars" }` | `(128)` |
| `small-whole` | `{ "value": "3", "size": "sm", "ariaLabel": "3 out of 5 stars" }` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "ariaLabel": "0 out of 5 stars", "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |
| `invalid-enum-falls-back` | `{ "value": "4.25", "ariaLabel": "0 out of 5 stars" }` (output equals `default`) | absent |
| `escaping` | `{ "ariaLabel": "Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | absent |

In addition the generator MUST emit one fixture per value named `value-<value with "." replaced by "-">` (`value-0`, `value-0-5`, `value-1`, `value-1-5`, `value-2`, `value-2-5`, `value-3`, `value-3-5`, `value-4`, `value-4-5`, `value-5`; 11 fixtures) with `ariaLabel` `<value> out of 5 stars`.

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `role` | stars | server | always | `img` |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: static markup.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: image with a text alternative (`role="img"` plus `aria-label` on the stars part); `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: `ariaLabel` (required) states the score in words. The count in the children is read after it as text; the caller SHOULD make it self-explanatory with a VisuallyHidden suffix (`(128<span class="rp-visually-hidden"> reviews</span>)`).
- Focus: not focusable. A Rating that links to the reviews is wrapped in a Link by the caller.
- Announcements: none.
- The filled stars use the current text colour and the unfilled stars use the same colour at 25 percent, so the display follows the scheme. In forced-colours mode the stars part is drawn in `CanvasText` and the score remains available as the accessible name.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/rating/rating.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--text-sm`, `--text-base`, `--text-lg`, `--rp-dir`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `--rp-rating-size` | `var(--text-base)` | block size of one star; the stars part is five times as wide |
| `--rp-rating-gap` | `calc(var(--spacing) * 1.5)` | gap between the stars and the count |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| `.rp-rating[data-value="0.5"]` | `--_fill: 10%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="1"]` | `--_fill: 20%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="1.5"]` | `--_fill: 30%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="2"]` | `--_fill: 40%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="2.5"]` | `--_fill: 50%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="3"]` | `--_fill: 60%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="3.5"]` | `--_fill: 70%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="4"]` | `--_fill: 80%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="4.5"]` | `--_fill: 90%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-value="5"]` | `--_fill: 100%` |
| `.rp-rating[data-size="sm"]` | `--rp-rating-size: var(--text-sm)` |
| `.rp-rating[data-size="lg"]` | `--rp-rating-size: var(--text-lg)` |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-rating` | custom properties: the two public properties above; `--_fill: 0%`. Declarations: `display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--rp-rating-gap); font-size: var(--rp-rating-size); line-height: 1; vertical-align: middle` |
| PARTS | `.rp-rating-stars` | `display: inline-block; flex: none; inline-size: 5em; block-size: 1em; background-image: linear-gradient(calc(90deg * var(--rp-dir)), currentColor var(--_fill), color-mix(in oklab, currentColor 25%, transparent) var(--_fill)); mask-image: url("<STAR>"); mask-size: 20% 100%; mask-repeat: repeat-x; mask-position: 0 0` |
| ENVIRONMENT | `.rp-rating-stars` (inside `@media (forced-colors: active)`) | `forced-color-adjust: none; background-image: linear-gradient(calc(90deg * var(--rp-dir)), CanvasText var(--_fill), GrayText var(--_fill))` |

`<STAR>` MUST be exactly this data URI (one line; a solid star in a 24 by 24 box; no colour is named because the default SVG fill is used only as mask alpha):

```text
data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 24 24'%3E%3Cpath d='M12 2.5l2.9 6 6.6.9-4.8 4.6 1.2 6.5-5.9-3.2-5.9 3.2 1.2-6.5-4.8-4.6 6.6-.9z'/%3E%3C/svg%3E
```

Notes:

1. The gradient angle `calc(90deg * var(--rp-dir))` is `90deg` (fill grows from the left) in left-to-right and `-90deg` (fill grows from the right) in right-to-left, so the stars fill from the inline start. The mask is symmetric, so `mask-position` needs no mirroring.
2. The unprefixed `mask-*` properties are used. No `-webkit-mask-*` duplicate is written (`03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 12, browser floor).
3. The count text takes the font size of the root (`--rp-rating-size`). A caller that wants a different count size wraps the count in Text.

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Rating(props: RatingProps)`; generated; `value?: number | RatingValue` where `RatingValue` is the string union of the Values column; the binding prints `String(value)`.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'rating', value: v, aria_label: label %}({{ count }}){% endblock %}`. Schema settings: `size` (`value` is data, not a merchant setting, and MUST NOT be a schema setting although it is an enum with a default). Locale keys: `ripple.rating.name`.

```tsx
<Rating value={Math.round(avg * 2) / 2} ariaLabel={`${avg.toFixed(1)} out of 5 stars`}>
  ({count})
</Rating>
```

```liquid
{% assign avg = product.metafields.reviews.rating.value.rating %}
{% assign v = avg | times: 2 | round | divided_by: 2.0 | remove: '.0' %}
{% capture label %}{{ 'products.rating_label' | t: rating: avg }}{% endcapture %}
{% block 'rating', value: v, aria_label: label %}({{ product.metafields.reviews.rating_count }}){% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4 (RP101 reports a literal `value` outside the list; RP104 reports a missing `ariaLabel`). Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: for each `value-*` fixture, the computed `--_fill` on the root equals `value * 20` percent, and the stars part computes inline size 80 px and block size 16 px at default dials (`size="md"`).
3. Tier 5: screenshot of fixture `with-count`: sampling the stars part on its vertical centre line, the pixel at 50 percent of the width of the fifth star's left half is opaque in the full colour and the pixel at the centre of its right half has lower alpha. Repeated inside `dir="rtl"`, the result is mirrored.
4. Tier 3: the accessibility snapshot of fixture `with-count` contains an image named `4.5 out of 5 stars` followed by the text `(128)`.
5. Tier 2: the React host given `value={4.5}` (number) and the Liquid host given `value: '4.5'` print the same `data-value="4.5"`; both given `4.25` print `data-value="0"`.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` section 8.1 (Rating), section 4.3 (enum props render as `data-<prop>`); `ripple/docs/03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 10 (`--rp-dir` for physical values), CSS-12 (`currentColor`, `color-mix()`, system colours in forced-colours media), CSS-06.
- Accepted by the CSS linter (D-105 item 2): a data-URI `mask-image`.
- UNVERIFIED: the Liquid float formatting assumed by `remove: '.0'` (that `8 | divided_by: 2.0` prints `4.0`); acceptance test 5 does not depend on it because it passes strings. UNVERIFIED: the metafield path in the Liquid example (example only).

### Fallback (SPIKE-06)

If the browser-floor spike of `03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 12 finds a floor browser without unprefixed `mask-image`, add the three `-webkit-mask-image`, `-webkit-mask-size`, `-webkit-mask-repeat` declarations before the unprefixed ones in the stars part rule, with the same values.

## SkipLink

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `skip-link` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `utilities` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `a` `.rp-skip-link` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

The first focusable element of the page: a link that is off-screen until it receives keyboard focus and that jumps past the header to the main content.

- Use when: writing a document shell. The `layout` blueprint (`12-components-chrome-and-blueprints.md`) prints exactly one, as the first child of `<body>`.
- Do not use when: linking anywhere else (use Link).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `SkipLink` | `blocks/skip-link.liquid` | `a` | `rp-skip-link` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: MUST be the first focusable element in `<body>`. The document MUST contain an element whose `id` equals the fragment of `href`; the `layout` blueprint of `12-components-chrome-and-blueprints.md` prints `<main id="main" tabindex="-1">` (Liquid: additionally `data-template="{{ request.page_type }}"`, D-099), so the default `href="#main"` always resolves (D-086).

### Props

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `href` | `href` | url | `#` followed by an element id | `#main` | no | no | `href` |

SkipLink takes the universal props `id` and `class`; it does NOT take `span` or `hidden` (it is out of flow).

### Content model

`text` - the link text, required, for example `Skip to content`. The text is the caller's (a theme translation); Ripple ships no locale key for it (FC-8).

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props; children `Skip to content`):

```html
<a class="rp-skip-link" href="#main">Skip to content</a>
```

Fixture `custom-target` (`href="#results"`; children `Skip to results`):

```html
<a class="rp-skip-link" href="#results">Skip to results</a>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `Skip to content` |
| `custom-target` | `{ "href": "#results" }` | `Skip to results` |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | `Skip to content` |
| `escaping` | `{ "href": "#Say \"hi\" & <go>" }` | `Skip to content` |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

None.

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: a same-document fragment link. Activating it scrolls to the target and moves the sequential focus starting point there; with `tabindex="-1"` on the target, focus moves to the target.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: skip link (WCAG 2.4.1 Bypass Blocks); `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: the link text.
- Focus: focusable; first in the tab order. Visible only while it matches `:focus-visible`; it then shows the global focus ring (`03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 11).
- Announcements: none.
- The link is moved off-screen with `translate`, not hidden with `display` or `visibility`, so that it stays in the tab order and in the accessibility tree.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| `Tab` (first press on the page) | focuses the link and reveals it |
| `Enter` | follows the link to the target |

### Styling

- File: `components/skip-link/skip-link.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: `--spacing`, `--spacing-control-md`, `--radius-control`, `--text-sm`, `--font-weight-medium`, `--color-background`, `--color-foreground`, `--color-border`, `--shadow-md`, `--rp-z-toast`, `--rp-duration-fast`, `--ease-out`.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| none | | |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| none | |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-skip-link` | `position: absolute; inset-block-start: calc(var(--spacing) * 2); inset-inline-start: calc(var(--spacing) * 2); z-index: var(--rp-z-toast); display: inline-flex; align-items: center; box-sizing: border-box; min-block-size: var(--spacing-control-md); padding-block: 0; padding-inline: calc(var(--spacing) * 4); border: 1px solid var(--color-border); border-radius: var(--radius-control); background-color: var(--color-background); color: var(--color-foreground); box-shadow: var(--shadow-md); font-size: var(--text-sm); font-weight: var(--font-weight-medium); text-decoration: none; translate: 0 -200%` |
| STATES | `.rp-skip-link:focus-visible` | `translate: 0 0` |
| MOTION | `.rp-skip-link` (inside the motion media query) | `transition: translate var(--rp-duration-fast) var(--ease-out)` |

`translate: 0 -200%` moves the link up by twice its own height, which with the 8 px block offset places it wholly above the viewport. It does not cause horizontal overflow in either direction.

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function SkipLink(props: SkipLinkProps)`; generated; `href = "#main"` default.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'skip-link' %}{{ 'accessibility.skip_to_content' | t }}{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: none. Locale keys: `ripple.skip-link.name`.

```tsx
<body>
  <SkipLink>Skip to content</SkipLink>
  <Header />
  <main id="main" tabIndex={-1}>
    <Outlet />
  </main>
</body>
```

```liquid
<body>
  {% block 'skip-link' %}{{ 'accessibility.skip_to_content' | t }}{% endblock %}
  {% block 'header' %}{% endblock %}
  <main id="main" tabindex="-1" data-template="{{ request.page_type }}">{{ content_for_layout }}</main>
</body>
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4. Manifest `rules[]`: none. (That the target id exists is checked per blueprint by the test in `12-components-chrome-and-blueprints.md`, not by the static checker, because the target is in another file.)

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 5: fixture `default` as the first child of `<body>` followed by `<main id="main" tabindex="-1">`: before any key press the link's bounding box bottom is less than or equal to 0; after one `Tab` press `document.activeElement` is the link and its bounding box is inside the viewport with top and inline-start offsets of 8 px at default dials.
3. Tier 5: after `Tab` then `Enter`, `location.hash` is `#main` and `document.activeElement` is the `main` element.
4. Tier 5: the same in a `dir="rtl"` document: the focused link's right edge is 8 px from the viewport's right edge.
5. Tier 5: `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` equals `clientWidth` before and after focus (no horizontal overflow).

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-086 (SkipLink `href` defaults to `#main`; the layout's `<main>` has `id="main"`), D-099 item 3 (`<main id="main" data-template="{{ request.page_type }}">` in the Liquid layout), section 8.1 (SkipLink), section 8.5 (`layout` blueprint lists SkipLink and `<main id="main" data-template>`); `ripple/docs/02-html-contract.md` A-10 ("SkipLink targets the `id` of `<main>`"); `ripple/docs/03-styling-and-tokens.md` section 4.1 (`:where(dialog, main):focus-visible { outline: none }`), CSS-19 (`z-index` values).
- UNVERIFIED: the theme translation key `accessibility.skip_to_content` in the Liquid example (example only; the text is the caller's).

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.

## VisuallyHidden

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `visually-hidden` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `utilities` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `span` `.rp-visually-hidden` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

Text that assistive technology reads and that is not painted: extra context for a link or a count ("items in cart", "reviews").

- Use when: a visible label is too terse out of context and an `aria-label` would replace, not extend, the visible text.
- Do not use when: the element is a control with no visible text (use `ariaLabel` on the control), content must be hidden from everyone (use the `hidden` attribute), or content must be hidden at some breakpoints (use the `hidden` prop).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `VisuallyHidden` | `blocks/visually-hidden.liquid` | `span` | `rp-visually-hidden` | none | yes | none |

Nesting rules: MUST NOT contain focusable elements (a focused, unpainted control fails WCAG 2.4.7).

### Props

VisuallyHidden takes the universal props `id` and `class` only; it does NOT take `span` or `hidden`.

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| none | | | | | | | |

### Content model

`text` - phrasing content with no interactive descendants.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props; children `items in cart`):

```html
<span class="rp-visually-hidden">items in cart</span>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | `items in cart` |
| `with-id` | `{ "id": "cart-count-label" }` | `items in cart` |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | `items in cart` |

Host-owned regions and masks: None.

### State and attributes

None.

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

None.

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional: static markup.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: visually hidden text; `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: not applicable; the text contributes to the name of an enclosing link or button and is read in place otherwise.
- Focus: not focusable; MUST NOT contain focusable content.
- Announcements: none.

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/visually-hidden/visually-hidden.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: none.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| none | | |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| none | |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-visually-hidden` | `position: absolute; inline-size: 1px; block-size: 1px; margin: calc(1px * -1); padding: 0; overflow: hidden; border: 0; clip-path: inset(50%); white-space: nowrap` |

The class `rp-visually-hidden` MAY also be written by hand on any element (it is the documented way to hide a `<legend>` or a `<caption>` visually); the checker accepts it on any element (exception to RP110 for this one class).

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function VisuallyHidden(props: VisuallyHiddenProps)`; generated.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'visually-hidden' %}<children>{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: none. Locale keys: `ripple.visually-hidden.name`.

<Link href="/cart">
  Cart (3<VisuallyHidden> items</VisuallyHidden>)
</Link>
```

{% block 'visually-hidden' %}{{ 'cart.items_label' | t }}{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4, with the RP110 exception stated under Styling. Manifest `rules[]`: none.

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass. (For CA-5 the compared box is 1 by 1 px.)
2. Tier 5: fixture `default` computes `position: absolute`, a bounding box of 1 by 1 px, and `clip-path: inset(50%)`; a screenshot of a page that contains only the fixture on the page background has every pixel equal to the background colour.
3. Tier 3: the accessibility snapshot of `<a class="rp-link" href="/cart" data-variant="inline">Cart (3<span class="rp-visually-hidden"> items</span>)</a>` has a link named `Cart (3 items)`.
4. Tier 5: a 200 px wide `overflow: auto` container that holds the fixture has `scrollWidth` equal to `clientWidth`.

## Announcer

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Id | `announcer` |
| Kind | `static` |
| Category | `utilities` |
| Custom elements | none |
| Root element and class | `div` `.rp-announcer` |
| Liquid tier | `block` |
| No-JS level | `full` |
| Requires `id` | no |
| Depends on | none |
| Milestone | M1 |

### Purpose

An empty, visually hidden live region placed inside a modal dialog or an `rp-*` root (D-079). The runtime function `announce(text, { from })` writes status text into the first `[data-rp-part="status"]` element of the open modal `<dialog>` that contains `from`, because content outside an open modal dialog is inert and a page-level live region is not announced (D-008 item 7).

- Use when: building a Dialog or Drawer body, or a commerce root, whose script-driven changes must be announced. A Dialog or Drawer MAY print one directly after its header (`10-components-interactive.md`). CartLines, CartSummary, CartNote and DiscountCode always print their own status part (D-079 item 3; `11-components-commerce.md`), so an author does not add an Announcer to them.
- Do not use when: the announcement is page-level (the runtime creates its own page-level regions; `04-elements.md` section 3), or the message must be visible (use FormMessage or Banner).

### Anatomy

| Part | Export (TSX) | Liquid file | Element | Class | Hook attribute | Required | Id rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| root | `Announcer` | `blocks/announcer.liquid` | `div` | `rp-announcer` | `data-rp-part="status"` | yes | none |

Nesting rules:

1. MUST be a descendant of a `<dialog>` or of an `rp-*` root.
2. At most one per `<dialog>` and at most one per `rp-*` root (not counting those of nested dialogs).
3. MUST NOT be placed directly in `<body>` or anywhere outside a `<dialog>` and outside every `rp-*` root: the `layout` blueprint prints NO Announcer; page-level live regions are created by the runtime (D-079 item 2, `00-decisions.md` section 8.5).

### Props

Announcer takes the universal props `id` and `class` only; it does NOT take `span` or `hidden`.

| Prop (TSX) | Liquid param | Type | Values | Default | Required | Responsive | Renders as |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| none | | | | | | | |

### Content model

`none` - the root is printed empty. The server MUST NOT print text in it.

### HTML contract

Fixture `default` (no props):

```html
<div class="rp-announcer" role="status" data-rp-part="status"></div>
```

| Fixture | `props` | `children` |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `{}` | absent |
| `class` | `{ "class": "custom-a custom-b" }` | absent |

Host-owned regions and masks: the text content of the root is runtime-owned after load. It is empty in every fixture, so no mask is needed.

### State and attributes

| Attribute | On | Written by | When | Initial value printed by server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `role` | root | server | always | `status` |
| `data-rp-part` | root | server | always | `status` |

Upgrade allow-list (mutations permitted when the element upgrades): None at upgrade. After upgrade, `announce()` MAY replace the text content of the root (`04-elements.md` section 3); no attribute is written.

### Behaviour with JavaScript

The component has no script of its own (FC-1). The runtime's `announce(text, { from })` looks for the nearest enclosing open modal `<dialog>` of `from` that contains `[data-rp-part="status"]` and writes the text there; otherwise it uses the page-level regions (`04-elements.md` section 3, rule 2).

### Behaviour without JavaScript

Fully functional in the sense of D-005: the region is empty and inert; nothing depends on it. Server-rendered feedback uses FormMessage or Banner.

### Events, commands and methods

None.

### Accessibility

- Pattern: live region (`role="status"`, polite); `checkPattern`: none.
- Accessible name: not applicable.
- Focus: not focusable.
- Announcements: whatever `announce()` writes. Errors raised inside a dialog use a visible FormMessage with `role="alert"` in addition.
- The region is visually hidden with the same declarations as VisuallyHidden; it MUST NOT be hidden with `display: none`, `visibility: hidden` or the `hidden` attribute (a hidden live region is not announced).

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| none | none |

### Styling

- File: `components/announcer/announcer.css` (layer `rp.components`).
- Tokens used: none.

| Custom property | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| none | | |

| Modifier selector | Sets |
|---|---|
| none | |

| File section | Selector | Declarations |
|---|---|---|
| ROOT | `.rp-announcer` | `position: absolute; inline-size: 1px; block-size: 1px; margin: calc(1px * -1); padding: 0; overflow: hidden; border: 0; clip-path: inset(50%); white-space: nowrap` |

The declarations are repeated here, not shared with VisuallyHidden, because a component file styles only its own class (CSS-02).

### Host notes

- HTML: None.
- React: `export function Announcer(props: AnnouncerProps)`; generated. The element has no children in React, so React never reconciles the text the runtime writes.
- Remix: None.
- Liquid: `{% block 'announcer' %}{% endblock %}`. Schema settings: none. Locale keys: `ripple.announcer.name`.

```tsx
<Dialog id="size-guide">
  <DialogBody>
    <Announcer />
    {children}
  </DialogBody>
</Dialog>
```

```liquid
{% block 'announcer' %}{% endblock %}
```

### Commerce

None.

### Agent surface

`accessibility-tree`; actions: none; confirm: none; secret: none.

### Checker rules

Common rules of section 0.4. Manifest `rules[]` (reported by RP113, D-059): `announcer-needs-container`: kind `requires-ancestor`, selector `:scope`, target `dialog, rp-dialog, rp-cart, rp-product-form, rp-live, rp-predictive-search, rp-recommendations, rp-quantity, rp-carousel, rp-navigation-menu` (a `<dialog>` and every `rp-*` component tag of `00-decisions.md` section 8; `rp-view-event` is not a container). Message: "An Announcer is outside a dialog and outside every rp-* root." Fix: "Move the Announcer inside the dialog, or delete it: page-level announcements need no markup."

### Acceptance tests

1. CA-1 to CA-9 pass.
2. Tier 2: every fixture output has `role="status"`, `data-rp-part="status"` and no child nodes.
3. Tier 5: fixture `default` computes a bounding box of 1 by 1 px and `display` other than `none`.
4. Tier 3: a page with `<dialog id="d"><button id="b" type="button">x</button>` + fixture `default` + `</dialog>`, opened with `showModal()`: after `announce("Added to cart", { from: document.getElementById("b") })` the fixture root's `textContent` is `Added to cart` and no page-level region contains that text.
5. Tier 4: React server render then hydration of `<Announcer />`, then `announce()` writes text, then the parent re-renders: the text remains and no hydration warning is logged.
6. Tier 1: fixture `default` as a direct child of `<body>` produces exactly one RP113 finding whose problem ends with `[announcer-needs-container]`; the same fixture inside `<dialog>` and inside `<rp-cart>` produces none.

### Evidence and UNVERIFIED

- Evidence: `ripple/docs/00-decisions.md` D-079 (root markup, placement, no Announcer in `layout`), D-008 item 7, section 8.1 (`div role="status" data-rp-part="status"`), section 8.5; `ripple/docs/04-elements.md` section 3 rule 2 (`data-rp-part="status"` inside an open modal dialog), section 2 (`src/runtime/announcer.ts`).
- UNVERIFIED: None. (The signature `announce(text, { from })` is fixed by D-019 item 5 and D-079.)

### Fallback (SPIKE-nn)

None.
